# Preface<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002222115730"></a>

**Overview<a name="section4537382116410"></a>**

This document mainly describes the installation and usage of the IDE tool. The IDE tool used is HiSpark Studio, which is mainly used for code editing, compilation, and image burning.

**Intended Audience<a name="section4378592816410"></a>**

This document mainly applies to the following engineers:

-   Technical support engineer
-   Hardware development engineer

# Tool Introduction<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378877440"></a>

The HiSpark Studio for VS Code plugin provides a one-stop integrated development environment for smart device developers. It provides developers with functions such as code editing, compilation, burning, and debugging, and supports the C/C++ language and 64-bit Windows 10 or Windows 11 operating systems. The plugin has the following features:

-   Supports code search, code highlighting, automatic code completion, code input hints, and code checking, allowing developers to code easily and efficiently.
-   Supports step-by-step debugging and viewing debugging information such as memory, variables, and call stacks.
-   Supports automatically detecting whether the toolchains required by each chip/development board are complete, and provides one-click download and installation of missing toolchains.

The HiSpark Studio for VS Code plugin is mainly divided into the following six functional areas, as shown in [Figure 1](#zh-cn_topic_0000002293219986_fig19904573445).

① WELCOME: Provides options such as the welcome page, usage guide, and creating/importing projects.

② PROJECT EXPLORER: Project area file display area

③ COMMANDS: Provides function buttons such as new project, open project, clean, compile, and burn, and controls the display/hiding of buttons in the status bar

④ Code editing area: Provides functions such as code viewing, editing, navigation, and highlighting.

⑤ Output console: Provides functions such as printing operation logs, entering debugging commands, and command-line tools.

⑥ Status bar: Provides commonly used function buttons, including new project, import project, project configuration, clean, compile, and burn, and displays information such as the encoding format, line count, and column count of the current file.

**Figure 1**  Functional area diagram<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293219986_fig19904573445"></a>  
![](figures/功能分区图.png "Functional area diagram")

The chips currently supported by the HiSpark Studio for VS Code plugin and their corresponding features are shown in [Table 1](#zh-cn_topic_0000002293219986_table82241939151116).

>Among the chip series, "Short-range IoT", "Wearables", and "Wide-area IoT" all belong to the FBB series. In this document, FBB series chips refer to these three chip series.

# Development Environment Setup<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412436853"></a>







## Overview<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717588"></a>

The current version supports only Windows 10 and Windows 11 systems. This chapter mainly introduces how to set up the development environment on Windows 10 and Windows 11 systems.

## Installation Requirements<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412317005"></a>

-   OS requirement: Supports 64-bit Windows 10 or 64-bit Windows 11.
-   VS Code version requirement: 1.80.2-1.103.2.
-   Disk space requirement: At least 900 MB of disk space is required to install the HiSpark Studio for VS Code plugin.
-   Memory requirement: The HiSpark Studio for VS Code plugin requires at least 1 GB RAM. It is recommended to have at least 4 GB RAM to install and run the HiSpark Studio for VS Code plugin.
-   CPU: The HiSpark Studio for VS Code plugin requires a processor of at least 1.6 GHz or higher.
-   C drive space requirement: It is recommended to have at least 1 GB of free space on the C drive.

## Installing the HiSpark Studio for VS Code Plugin<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378877444"></a>

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>If DevEco-related plugins have been installed previously, manually disable or uninstall all DevEco-related plugins; otherwise, they will conflict with the functions of the HiSpark Studio for VS Code plugin.

1.  Open the VS Code extension market and search for HiSpark Studio in the search box.

    **Figure 1**  VS Code extension market search box<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293219990_fig155444514303"></a>  
    ![](figures/VS-Code插件市场搜索框.png "VS Code extension market search box")

2.  In the search results, select HiSpark Studio for VS Code and click Install.

## Toolchain Configuration<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412436857"></a>

The HiSpark Studio for VS Code plugin depends on the toolchain, Python, and pip.pyz dependency environments when performing operations such as compiling projects. You can use the Download Toolchain function in the plugin to automatically download and install the toolchain and other components.

**Figure 1**  Download Toolchain function<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002337256433_fig4608724245"></a>  
![](figures/Download-Toolchain功能.png "Download Toolchain function")

After clicking the Download Toolchain function, python3.11.4, pip.pyz, the dependencies of pip.pyz (wheel, setuptools, cmake, kconfiglib, pycparser, pillow, numpy, opencv\_python, windows\_curses, and tkinter-embed), and the tools required for compilation are downloaded in sequence. During the download, the notification bar in the lower right corner of VS Code shows the corresponding download prompts and progress. Because the compiler file is large, the download takes a long time; please wait patiently.

**Figure 2**  Toolchain download progress (partial)<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002337256433_fig13993195610574"></a>  
![](figures/Toolchain下载进度展示（部分）.png "Toolchain download progress (partial)")

After the download is complete, the downloaded content is located in the "..\\.vscode\\extensions\\hispark.hisparkstudio-X.X.X\\downloads" directory under the user directory.

**Figure 3**  Toolchain download directory<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002337256433_fig1964666125817"></a>  
![](figures/Toolchain下载目录.png "Toolchain download directory")

After python3.11.4 is installed, the dependencies of pip.pyz are installed and the compilation toolchain is decompressed. Corresponding notification pop-ups appear in the lower right corner, and when all installations are complete, a prompt indicates that the environment preparation is complete.

**Figure 4**  Decompression prompts for pip.pyz dependencies and toolchain after python3.11.4 installation<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002337256433_fig7778922205811"></a>  
![](figures/python3-11-4安装完成后的pip-pyz依赖及工具链的解压提示.png "Decompression prompts for pip.pyz dependencies and toolchain after python3.11.4 installation")

During environment preparation, the zip package downloaded to "....\\.vscode\\extensions\\hispark.hisparkstudio-X.X.X\\downloads\\HiSparkStudioToolchain.zip" under the user directory is decompressed to "..\\.vscode\\extensions\\hispark.hisparkstudio-X.X.X\\tools" under the user directory.

**Figure 5**  Compilation toolchain decompression result<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002337256433_fig136041725175413"></a>  
![](figures/编译工具链解压结果.png "Compilation toolchain decompression result")

The Python environment can be verified by opening a cmd (Command Prompt) window under the "..\\.vscode\\extensions\\hispark.hisparkstudio-X.X.X\\tools\\python" path in the user directory. If running ".\\python.exe --version" outputs 'Python 3.11.4', and running ".\\python.exe C:\\username\\.vscode\\extensions\\hispark.hisparkstudio-X.X.X\\downloads\\pip.pyz list" displays the pip.pyz dependencies (wheel, setuptools, cmake, kconfiglib, pycparser, pillow, numpy, opencv\_python, windows\_curses, and tkinter-embed) and their versions, the Python environment is verified to be correct.

**Figure 6**  Python environment verification<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002337256433_fig1757924515812"></a>  
![](figures/python环境验证.png "Python environment verification")

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>If automatic toolchain environment configuration fails due to environmental or other reasons, you can also configure it manually:
>-   Python manual download link: [https://mirrors.huaweicloud.com/python/3.11.4/python-3.11.4-embed-amd64.zip](https://mirrors.huaweicloud.com/python/3.11.4/python-3.11.4-embed-amd64.zip). Decompress this file to the "..\\.vscode\\extensions\\hispark.hisparkstudio-X.X.X\\tools" directory (the tools directory must be created manually), and name the decompressed file python.
>    ![](figures/python安装3.png)
>-   Download the "pip.pyz" file to the "..\\.vscode\\extensions\\hispark.hisparkstudio-X.X.X\\tools\\python" directory, and copy the downloaded "pip.pyz" file to the "..\\.vscode\\extensions\\hispark.hisparkstudio-X.X.X\\downloads" directory. If the downloads directory does not exist, create a downloads folder manually.
>    pip.pyz：[https://bootstrap.pypa.io/pip/pip.pyz](https://bootstrap.pypa.io/pip/pip.pyz)
>-   Modify the "..\\.vscode\\extensions\\hispark.hisparkstudio-X.X.X\\tools\\python\\python311.\_pth" file and delete the \# before "\#import site".
>    ![](figures/python安装4.png)
>-   To prepare the pip.pyz dependencies, download the whl files of the corresponding dependencies to the "..\\.vscode\\extensions\\hispark.hisparkstudio-X.X.X\\tools\\python" directory, open cmd in that directory, and run the ".\\python.exe pip.pyz install xx.whl" command. For example, if the cmake whl file is "cmake-3.20.5-py2.py3-none-win\_amd64.whl", open cmd in the download directory and run ".\\python.exe pip.pyz install cmake-3.20.5-py2.py3-none-win\_amd64.whl". The download links of the whl files of each dependency are as follows:
>    wheel：
>    [https://mirrors.cloud.tencent.com/pypi/packages/0b/2c/87f3254fd8ffd29e4c02732eee68a83a1d3c346ae39bc6822dcbcb697f2b/wheel-0.45.1-py3-none-any.whl](https://mirrors.cloud.tencent.com/pypi/packages/0b/2c/87f3254fd8ffd29e4c02732eee68a83a1d3c346ae39bc6822dcbcb697f2b/wheel-0.45.1-py3-none-any.whl)
>    setuptools：
>    [https://mirrors.cloud.tencent.com/pypi/packages/a3/dc/17031897dae0efacfea57dfd3a82fdd2a2aeb58e0ff71b77b87e44edc772/setuptools-80.9.0-py3-none-any.whl](https://mirrors.cloud.tencent.com/pypi/packages/a3/dc/17031897dae0efacfea57dfd3a82fdd2a2aeb58e0ff71b77b87e44edc772/setuptools-80.9.0-py3-none-any.whl)
>    cmake：
>    [https://mirrors.cloud.tencent.com/pypi/packages/65/7f/80cf681cd376834b442af8af48e6f17b4197d20b7255aa2f76d8d93a9e44/cmake-3.20.5-py2.py3-none-win\_amd64.whl](https://mirrors.cloud.tencent.com/pypi/packages/65/7f/80cf681cd376834b442af8af48e6f17b4197d20b7255aa2f76d8d93a9e44/cmake-3.20.5-py2.py3-none-win_amd64.whl)
>    kconfiglib：
>    [https://mirrors.cloud.tencent.com/pypi/packages/8a/f1/d98a89231e779b079b977590efcc31249d959c8f1d4b5858cad69695ff9c/kconfiglib-14.1.0-py2.py3-none-any.whl](https://mirrors.cloud.tencent.com/pypi/packages/8a/f1/d98a89231e779b079b977590efcc31249d959c8f1d4b5858cad69695ff9c/kconfiglib-14.1.0-py2.py3-none-any.whl)
>    pycparser：
>    [https://mirrors.cloud.tencent.com/pypi/packages/62/d5/5f610ebe421e85889f2e55e33b7f9a6795bd982198517d912eb1c76e1a53/pycparser-2.21-py2.py3-none-any.whl](https://mirrors.cloud.tencent.com/pypi/packages/62/d5/5f610ebe421e85889f2e55e33b7f9a6795bd982198517d912eb1c76e1a53/pycparser-2.21-py2.py3-none-any.whl)
>    pillow：
>    [https://mirrors.cloud.tencent.com/pypi/packages/c1/d0/5866318eec2b801cdb8c82abf190c8343d8a1cd8bf5a0c17444a6f268291/pillow-10.4.0-cp311-cp311-win\_amd64.whl](https://mirrors.cloud.tencent.com/pypi/packages/c1/d0/5866318eec2b801cdb8c82abf190c8343d8a1cd8bf5a0c17444a6f268291/pillow-10.4.0-cp311-cp311-win_amd64.whl)
>    numpy：
>    [https://mirrors.cloud.tencent.com/pypi/packages/9b/0f/022ca4783b6e6239a53b988a4d315d67f9ae7126227fb2255054a558bd72/numpy-2.0.0-cp311-cp311-win\_amd64.whl](https://mirrors.cloud.tencent.com/pypi/packages/9b/0f/022ca4783b6e6239a53b988a4d315d67f9ae7126227fb2255054a558bd72/numpy-2.0.0-cp311-cp311-win_amd64.whl)
>    opencv\_python：
>    [https://mirrors.cloud.tencent.com/pypi/packages/fa/80/eb88edc2e2b11cd2dd2e56f1c80b5784d11d6e6b7f04a1145df64df40065/opencv\_python-4.12.0.88-cp37-abi3-win\_amd64.whl](https://mirrors.cloud.tencent.com/pypi/packages/fa/80/eb88edc2e2b11cd2dd2e56f1c80b5784d11d6e6b7f04a1145df64df40065/opencv_python-4.12.0.88-cp37-abi3-win_amd64.whl)
>    windows\_curses：
>    [https://mirrors.cloud.tencent.com/pypi/packages/18/1b/e06eb41dad1c74f0d3124218084f258f73a5e76c67112da0ba174162670f/windows\_curses-2.3.3-cp311-cp311-win\_amd64.whl](https://mirrors.cloud.tencent.com/pypi/packages/18/1b/e06eb41dad1c74f0d3124218084f258f73a5e76c67112da0ba174162670f/windows_curses-2.3.3-cp311-cp311-win_amd64.whl)
>-   Download the tkinter package to the "..\\.vscode\\extensions\\hispark.hisparkstudio-X.X.X\\tools\\python" directory, open cmd in that directory, and run the ".\\python.exe pip.pyz install --target .\\ tkinter\_embed-3.11.0.tar.gz" command
>    tkinter package:
>    [https://mirrors.cloud.tencent.com/pypi/packages/a2/b5/01fa4f6b1b78b01c1602d8e6e28879dcbef2d399d934f28d3324c1114552/tkinter\_embed-3.11.0.tar.gz](https://mirrors.cloud.tencent.com/pypi/packages/a2/b5/01fa4f6b1b78b01c1602d8e6e28879dcbef2d399d934f28d3324c1114552/tkinter_embed-3.11.0.tar.gz)
>-   Add "toolChain.json" to the "..\\.vscode\\extensions\\hispark.hisparkstudio-X.X.X\\tools" directory and add the Python path content to "toolChain.json".
>    ```
>    {
>        "pythonDir": "..\\.vscode\\extensions\\hispark.hisparkstudio-X.X.X\\tools\\python"
>    }
>    ```
>    ![](figures/python安装5.png)
>-   The toolchain can be downloaded manually via the [https://hispark-obs.obs.cn-east-3.myhuaweicloud.com/HiSparkStudioToolchain.zip](https://hispark-obs.obs.cn-east-3.myhuaweicloud.com/HiSparkStudioToolchain.zip) link and then decompressed to the "..\\.vscode\\extensions\\hispark.hisparkstudio-X.X.X\\tools" directory.
>-   For verification of the toolchain environment and Python environment, refer to [Figure 5](#zh-cn_topic_0000002337256433_fig136041725175413) and [Figure 6](#zh-cn_topic_0000002337256433_fig1757924515812).

## SDK Download<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717596"></a>

Creating a project in the HiSpark Studio for VS Code plugin requires the SDK software package. The plugin currently provides SDK downloads for the WS63 and BS2X series.

**Figure 1**  Download SDK from HiSpark function<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002303416852_fig16959181457"></a>  
![](figures/Download-SDK-from-HiSpark功能.png "Download SDK from HiSpark function")

After clicking "Download SDK from HiSpark", the SDK download list is displayed.

**Figure 2**  SDK download list<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002303416852_fig862702418516"></a>  
![](figures/SDK下载列表.png "SDK download list")

After selecting any SDK, the "Select SDK save location" option is displayed.

**Figure 3**  SDK save location selection<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002303416852_fig109016331355"></a>  
![](figures/SDK保存位置选择.png "SDK save location selection")

After the save folder is selected, a pop-up in the lower right corner indicates that the SDK is being downloaded. If the waiting time is too long, it may be due to environmental reasons or lack of permission to download from gitee. In this case, manually download Git, add it to the environment variables, close the VS Code window and reopen it to ensure the environment variables are added successfully, and then perform the download again.

Git download link: [https://github.com/git-for-windows/git/releases/download/v2.48.1.windows.1/Git-2.48.1-64-bit.exe](https://github.com/git-for-windows/git/releases/download/v2.48.1.windows.1/Git-2.48.1-64-bit.exe)

**Figure 4**  SDK download prompt box<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002303416852_fig1437317573111"></a>  
![](figures/下载SDK提示框.png "SDK download prompt box")

After the SDK download is complete, you can create a project based on the SDK.

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-notice.gif) **Notice:** 
>-   To prevent the SDK from affecting other folders after download, it is recommended to select an empty folder as the SDK save location.
>-   The WS63 SDK is downloaded online via git clone [https://gitee.com/HiSpark/fbb\_ws63.git](https://gitee.com/HiSpark/fbb_ws63.git). If you do not have permission to download gitee code, the download will take too long and eventually fail.
>-   The BS2X SDK is downloaded online via git clone [https://gitee.com/HiSpark/fbb\_bs2x.git](https://gitee.com/HiSpark/fbb_bs2x.git). If you do not have permission to download gitee code, the download will take too long and eventually fail.

## bash Environment Preparation<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412317009"></a>

This section applies only to Hi2131, Hi2131C, and 3322 projects that require the bash environment to be compiled.

To compile Hi2131, Hi2131C, or 3322 projects, you need to manually download Git and add the bash path to the environment variables.

Download link: [https://github.com/git-for-windows/git/releases/download/v2.48.1.windows.1/Git-2.48.1-64-bit.exe](https://github.com/git-for-windows/git/releases/download/v2.48.1.windows.1/Git-2.48.1-64-bit.exe)

If "bash.exe" exists by default under System32 of the Windows system, delete the "bash.exe" in the system directory and use the built-in "bash.exe" from the installed Git.

After completing the above steps, restart VS Code to make the new environment variables take effect, and then the project can be compiled normally.

**Figure 1**  Deleting the bash in the Windows system library<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002304004976_fig9843191112367"></a>  
![](figures/删除Windows系统库的bash.png "Deleting the bash in the Windows system library")

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>If you are prompted that you do not have permission when deleting bash in the Windows system library, right-click the bash.exe to be deleted and select "Properties". In the pop-up window, click the "Security" tab, then "Advanced", and then click the "Change" button under the "Owner" tab. Select a new owner (for example, the current user), and click "Apply" and "OK". Return to the "Security" tab and click "Edit". Assign appropriate permissions (such as Full Control, Read, etc.) to the added user or group. After clicking "Apply" and "OK", you can delete the selected bash.exe file.

**Figure 2**  Configuring the deletion permission of bash.exe<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002304004976_fig31821532142019"></a>  
![](figures/配置bash-exe的删除权限.png "Configuring the deletion permission of bash.exe")

![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002478125622.png)

**Figure 3**  bash path under Git<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002304004976_fig664202311363"></a>  
![](figures/Git下的bash路径.png "bash path under Git")

**Figure 4**  Adding to the environment variables<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002304004976_fig1687216294365"></a>  
![](figures/添加到环境变量中.png "Adding to the environment variables")

**Figure 5**  Ensuring that the bash under Git is called<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002304004976_fig14591538123614"></a>  
![](figures/保证Git下的bash被调用.png "Ensuring that the bash under Git is called")

# Project Management<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378877448"></a>



## Creating a Project<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412436861"></a>

1.  Open the HiSpark Studio for VS Code plugin, enter the welcome page, click "New Project", and enter the new project page.

    **Figure 1**  HiSpark Studio for VS Code plugin welcome page<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327179609_fig6514428113718"></a>  
    ![](figures/HiSpark-Studio-for-VS-Code插件欢迎页面.png "HiSpark Studio for VS Code plugin welcome page")

2.  Configure the project parameters on the interface shown in [Figure 2](#zh-cn_topic_0000002327179609_fig2784311111318), and click "Finish".

    **Figure 2**  New project window<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327179609_fig2784311111318"></a>  
    ![](figures/新建工程窗口.png "New project window")

    -   Chip: Select the name of the chip used by the project.
    -   Development board: Select the name of the development board used by the project. When you do not need to customize the development board, the chip name is used as the development board name by default.
    -   Project type: Select the type of the project to be created. There are normal projects and sample projects. Sample projects currently support only the WS63 chip.
    -   Project name: Enter the project name.
    -   Project path: Select the directory for storing the project files.
    -   Software package: Select the root directory of the software development package (SDK) folder used by the project.

    When the chip is WS63 and the project type is "sample project", the "sample" path and the "sample selection" option are added to the project interface, as shown in [Figure 3](#zh-cn_topic_0000002327179609_fig16606922141519).

    **Figure 3**  sample project creation page<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327179609_fig16606922141519"></a>  
    ![](figures/sample工程创建页面.png "sample project creation page")

    The sample path can be imported locally (the path directory should contain the "build\_config.json file"), or you can directly select an empty folder directory and click the download icon button. The sample files are automatically downloaded to the selected empty folder directory. Then click sample selection, and the interface shown in [Figure 4](#zh-cn_topic_0000002327179609_fig111743454160) is displayed.

    **Figure 4**  sample selection page<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327179609_fig111743454160"></a>  
    ![](figures/sample选择页面.png "sample selection page")

    After selecting the required sample, close the sample selection page. The name of the selected sample option is filled in "sample selection" on the new project page, as shown in [Figure 5](#zh-cn_topic_0000002327179609_fig9923152471712).

    >![](public_sys-resources/icon-caution.gif) **Caution:** 
    >The sample is downloaded online via git clone [https://gitee.com/HiSpark/fbb\_ws63.git](https://gitee.com/HiSpark/fbb_ws63.git). If you do not have permission to download gitee code, the download will take too long and eventually fail.

    **Figure 5**  Page after sample selection is complete<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327179609_fig9923152471712"></a>  
    ![](figures/sample选择完成后页面.png "Page after sample selection is complete")

    Then select an appropriate project name, project path, and WS63 software package, and click "Finish" to create the project.

3.  View the project creation result.

    The HiSpark Studio for VS Code plugin automatically opens the newly created project and displays the created project in the project list on the welcome page, as shown in [Figure 6](#zh-cn_topic_0000002327179609_fig551738161718).

    **Figure 6**  Project creation result page<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327179609_fig551738161718"></a>  
    ![](figures/工程创建结果页面.png "Project creation result page")

## Importing a Project<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717600"></a>

1.  Open the HiSpark Studio for VS Code plugin, enter the welcome page, click "Import Project", and enter the import project page.

    **Figure 1**  HiSpark Studio for VS Code plugin import project page<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293379666_fig19540125184016"></a>  
    ![](figures/HiSpark-Studio-for-VS-Code插件导入工程页面.png "HiSpark Studio for VS Code plugin import project page")

2.  Select the path to import from, and all projects under that path are found. Check the projects to be imported and click "Finish".

    **Figure 2**  Import project configuration page<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293379666_fig3640203910157"></a>  
    ![](figures/导入工程配置页面.png "Import project configuration page")

3.  After the project import is complete, the project folder is displayed in the project area, and the imported project is displayed in the project list on the welcome page.

    **Figure 3**  Project import complete<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293379666_fig1617152231818"></a>  
    ![](figures/导入工程完成.png "Project import complete")

# Project Configuration<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412317013"></a>

Project configuration is mainly used to configure project configuration items such as basic information, compilation, debugging, and burning. Click the "![](figures/config.png)" button to open the project configuration interface, as shown in [Figure 1](#zh-cn_topic_0000002327179613_fig672755464817).

**Figure 1**  Clicking the project configuration button<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327179613_fig672755464817"></a>  
![](figures/单击工程配置按钮.png "Clicking the project configuration button")





## Basic Information Configuration<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378877452"></a>

Click the "Basic Information" tab on the left side of the project configuration interface to enter the basic information configuration interface, as shown in [Figure 1](#zh-cn_topic_0000002293379670_fig18947542448). This interface contains the project chip series, development board model, and software package path. You can modify the software package path corresponding to the project.

**Figure 1**  Basic information configuration interface<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293379670_fig18947542448"></a>  
![](figures/基本信息配置界面.png "Basic information configuration interface")

-   In the basic series configuration interface, the Target selection box and the associated Target management function are available only for some FBB chips:

    BS20, BS20C, BS21, BS21E, BS21A, BS22, and BS26 support both Target selection and Target management. Other projects that contain Target support only Target selection.

-   The following guide applies only to the above chips that involve the Target function:
    1.  As shown in [Figure 2](#zh-cn_topic_0000002293379670_fig1942333014511), different build products can be generated by selecting different Targets during compilation.

        **Figure 2**  Target selection interface<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293379670_fig1942333014511"></a>  
        ![](figures/Target选择界面.png "Target selection interface")

    2.  Click "Target Management" below the Target selection box to enter the Target management interface, as shown in [Figure 3](#zh-cn_topic_0000002293379670_fig1648301431310).

        **Figure 3**  Target management interface<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293379670_fig1648301431310"></a>  
        ![](figures/Target管理界面.png "Target management interface")

        ① SDK default Targets that do not support editing or deletion.

        ② Targets that support compilation and deletion.

    3.  Click "Add" on the Target management interface to enter the add Target interface, as shown in [Figure 4](#zh-cn_topic_0000002293379670_fig13409104010130).

        **Figure 4**  Add Target interface<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293379670_fig13409104010130"></a>  
        ![](figures/添加Target界面.png "Add Target interface")

    4.  When you add or delete a Target and switch back to the Target option on the project configuration interface, the change takes effect synchronously. Taking adding a Target as an example, after a new Target named demo is added, as shown in [Figure 5](#zh-cn_topic_0000002293379670_fig899411161410), the Target drop-down box on the project configuration interface is also updated with the DEMO option synchronously, as shown in [Figure 6](#zh-cn_topic_0000002293379670_fig38170493913).

        **Figure 5**  Adding a Target named demo<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293379670_fig899411161410"></a>  
        ![](figures/添加名称为demo的Target.png "Adding a Target named demo")

        **Figure 6**  Target drop-down box updated with DEMO synchronously<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293379670_fig38170493913"></a>  
        ![](figures/Target下拉框同步更新DEMO.png "Target drop-down box updated with DEMO synchronously")

        As shown in [Figure 7](#zh-cn_topic_0000002293379670_fig8566412205415), the Target parameter macro information after configuration is generated in the ".config" file under the target\_config directory.

        **Figure 7**  Target configuration file<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293379670_fig8566412205415"></a>  
        ![](figures/Target配置文件.png "Target configuration file")

        Target supports selection and customization. You can select the required Target from the Target list by clicking the Target selection box, and you can also customize the Target and the compilation commands.

        **Figure 8**  Target customization<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293379670_fig94764713276"></a>  
        ![](figures/Target自定义.png "Target customization")

        >![](public_sys-resources/icon-notice.gif) **Notice:** 
        >-   Target supports selection and customization. Each time you customize a Target, you need to manually enter the complete custom Target and command. Secondary editing based on a selected or customized Target is not supported; that is, each time you customize a Target, you must enter and edit from the first character.
        >-   When customizing a Target, if the entered Target already exists in the list and you add other compilation commands on this basis, as long as the compilation passes normally, both debugging and burning can be executed normally.
        >-   When customizing a Target, if the entered Target is not in the list and you compile on this basis, if the compilation passes normally, the debugging, stack analysis, and image analysis functions will be affected because no elf and map files are bound. In addition, after customizing a Target that is not in the list and reopening the project configuration, a prompt indicating that launch.json is missing and affects the debugging function will appear. This is normal.

## Compiler Configuration<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412436865"></a>

Click the "Compiler" tab on the left side of the project configuration interface to enter the compiler configuration interface, as shown in [Figure 1](#zh-cn_topic_0000002327219389_fig177671416389), [Figure 2](#zh-cn_topic_0000002327219389_fig892832183814), [Figure 3](#zh-cn_topic_0000002327219389_fig1020319297387), and [Figure 4](#zh-cn_topic_0000002327219389_fig1514082619416). This interface allows you to configure items such as the compilation toolchain, compilation type, compilation options, compilation commands, compilation result file path, and static libraries used by the project.

**Figure 1**  Compiler configuration interface-1<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327219389_fig177671416389"></a>  

![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002373319477.png)

**Figure 2**  Compiler configuration interface-2<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327219389_fig892832183814"></a>  

![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002339286536.png)

**Figure 3**  Compiler configuration interface-3<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327219389_fig1020319297387"></a>  

![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002339284964.png)

**Figure 4**  Compiler configuration interface-4<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327219389_fig1514082619416"></a>  

![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002339129804.png)

![](figures/编译器配置界面-4.png "Compiler configuration interface-4")

-   Compilation toolchain: The toolchain used for compilation.
-   Specify the linker to use the C library in the toolchain: Specify the linker to use the C library in the toolchain.
-   Specify the compiler to use the C library in the toolchain: Specify the compiler to use the C library in the toolchain.
-   Compilation type: Divided into debug and release.
    -   debug generates a debugging symbol table and packages it into the image, which is convenient for debugging.
    -   release does not generate a debugging symbol table, reducing the image size and being used for production.

-   Floating-point constant type: Divided into float and double.
    -   float can improve the running speed of the program, but may cause precision loss, because single-precision floating-point numbers can only represent a limited range and precision of numbers.
    -   double can improve the calculation precision of floating-point operations, but occupies more memory space and also increases the time consumption.

-   Generate CRC: Used to control whether the CRC algorithm calculation result is inserted at the end of "target.bin" and "target.hex" during compilation.
-   Generate checksum (using the CRC32 algorithm): The CRC algorithm calculation result is not inserted into the executable file, but is saved in the "checksum\_list.txt" file in the same directory and printed during the compilation process.
-   Generate the symbol table file (target.symbol): After compilation, the "target.symbol" symbol table file can be generated from the "target.elf" file in the "out/bin" directory. The file is located in the "out/bin" directory.
-   Generate target.hex: Used to control whether "target.hex" is generated during compilation.
-   Parse the elf file for Live Watch: Used to control whether the global variables in the elf are parsed during compilation. The parsing results are used in the Live Watch function during debugging.
-   Generate analyzerJson for project analysis: Used to control whether the project is statically analyzed during compilation. The analysis results are used for stack analysis and image analysis.
-   Image padding.
    -   no: No padding.
    -   0: Fill the extra space with 0.
    -   1: Fill the extra space with 1.

-   Compilation optimization level: Compilation optimization options.
    -   O0: No optimization is performed. The correspondence between source code and binary code is the best, making it suitable for debugging scenarios.
    -   Os: Optimizes space usage, suitable for production release scenarios with small Flash space.
    -   O1\~3: Optimizes running performance. The O1, O2, and O3 optimization levels increase in sequence, suitable for production release scenarios that need to improve running performance.

-   Enable the stack protection function (this increases the memory and flash usage).
-   Treat warnings as errors.
-   Display warning information.
    -   Do not warn on unused functions: Ignore warnings issued for declared but unused static functions.
    -   Do not warn on unused labels: Ignore warnings issued for declared but unused labels.
    -   Do not warn on unused parameters: Ignore warnings issued for declared but unused parameters.
    -   Do not warn on unused variables: Ignore warnings issued for declared but unused variables.
    -   Do not warn on missing function prototypes: Ignore warnings issued when a function is used before it is declared.

-   Whether to generate static libraries: If selected, the selected files are compiled to generate static library files; if not selected, no static library files are generated during compilation.
    -   Static library name

        Enter the name of the static library, such as "demo". After compilation, "libdemo.a" is generated in the "out\\libs" directory.

    -   Static library source files

        Select the files or folders to be compiled into a static library.

    -   Static library dependency header files

        Select the header files that the static library compilation depends on. Files and folders are supported.

-   External static library path.

    Configure static library files that are not under the current project directory to participate in compilation.

-   External static library dependency.

    Configure the static library dependency headers that are not under the current project directory to participate in compilation.

-   Global macro definitions.

    Configure macro definitions that are valid throughout the entire project.

-   Execute before compilation.

    Customize 1 to 2 commands. The checked commands are executed before compilation. The built-in variable $P (current project path) can be used in the commands.

-   Execute after compilation.

    Customize 1 to 2 commands. The checked commands are executed after compilation. The built-in variable $P (current project path) can be used in the commands.

    >![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
    >The configuration is automatically saved and takes effect after modification. Modifications in the text input box are automatically saved and take effect when the input box loses focus.

## Flasher Configuration<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717604"></a>

Click the "Program Loading" tab on the left side of the project configuration interface to enter the program loading configuration interface, as shown in [Figure 1](#zh-cn_topic_0000002327179617_fig1851473982716). This interface supports configuring the burning transmission mode and the parameters corresponding to the transmission mode.

**Figure 1**  Program loading configuration interface<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327179617_fig1851473982716"></a>  
![](figures/程序加载配置界面.png "Program loading configuration interface")

-   Transmission mode: Select the data transmission mode. Different parameter configuration items appear for different transmission modes.
    -   serial: Select transmission through the serial port. As shown in [Figure 2](#zh-cn_topic_0000002327179617_fig58771855184310).

        -   Port
        -   Baud rate

        **Figure 2**  Interface for selecting the serial configuration<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327179617_fig58771855184310"></a>  
        
        ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002339310954.png)

    -   usb: Select to complete burning and upgrading through USB devices. As shown in [Figure 3](#zh-cn_topic_0000002327179617_fig10468263017). (The current usb mode supports BS20, BS20C, BS21, BS21A, BS21E, BS22, BS25, and BS26 series chips.)
        -   USB device list

            **Figure 3**  Interface for selecting the usb configuration<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327179617_fig10468263017"></a>  
            
            ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002373349237.png)

        -   As shown in the figure, when switching to usb mode, the default Bin file is modified, and only program loading is supported. Burning configuration is not supported.

            **Figure 4**  Changing the transmission mode to usb mode<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327179617_fig21511259311"></a>  
            ![](figures/传输方式改为usb模式.png "Changing the transmission mode to usb mode")

-   Bin file: Specifies the file to be burned.
-   Reset after burning: After the burning is complete, the board performs a soft reset.
-   Verify after burning: After burning, the burned file is read back and compared to verify the integrity of the file.

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>-   The configuration is automatically saved and takes effect after modification.

## Debugger Configuration<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412317017"></a>

Click the "Debugger" tab on the left side of the project configuration interface, and select the Jlink executable software in the JlinkGDBServerCL path, as shown in [Figure 1](#zh-cn_topic_0000002340080528_fig205561635163111).
