# Compilation and Running<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378877456"></a>





## Compilation Buttons<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412436869"></a>

**Figure 1**  Compilation buttons<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293383670_fig7871826336"></a>  
![](figures/编译按钮.png "Compilation buttons")

The buttons in [Figure 1](#zh-cn_topic_0000002293383670_fig7871826336) are, in order: Clean, Compile, Recompile, and Stop Compilation.

-   ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002339324210.png): Click to trigger project cleaning, which deletes the intermediate files generated during compilation.
-   ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002339323986.png): Click to trigger project compilation.
-   ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002373241301.png): Click to trigger cleaning the target first and then compiling.
-   ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002373362865.png): Click to trigger stopping the compilation.

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>-   The recompile function first clears the contents of the current target in the output directory, such as the folders named target in the acore directory and the fwpkg directory. If such a folder exists, it is deleted; if not, no files or folders are cleared. After the clearing is complete, the compilation function is executed again.
>-   To delete the output folder, simply execute the "Clean" function.

## Compilation Results<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717608"></a>

Click the "![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002373367797.png)" button to start compilation. After a successful compilation, the terminal window output is as shown in [Figure 1](#zh-cn_topic_0000002293224006_fig9925105200), and an output directory is generated in the project directory, as shown in [Figure 2](#zh-cn_topic_0000002293224006_fig114873277206).

**Figure 1**  Compilation successful<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293224006_fig9925105200"></a>  
![](figures/编译成功.png "Compilation successful")

**Figure 2**  Compilation generates the output directory<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293224006_fig114873277206"></a>  

![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002339329450.png)

Click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002339329878.png) button to start cleaning the files generated by compilation. After a successful cleaning, the terminal window output is as shown in [Figure 3](#zh-cn_topic_0000002293224006_fig1710335292011). Project cleaning clears the output folder in the project directory.

**Figure 3**  Project cleaning successful<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293224006_fig1710335292011"></a>  

![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002327183953.png)

## Compiling to Generate a Static Library<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412317021"></a>

Applicable scenario: You want to package some source files in the project to generate a static library file.

1.  Enter the project configuration page and go to the "Generate Static Library" configuration item.

    **Figure 1**  Static library configuration<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327223373_fig18878132542220"></a>  
    ![](figures/静态库配置.png "Static library configuration")

    Enable or disable generating a static library. If you do not want to generate a static library, you can turn it off again through this option.

2.  Enter the desired static library name. For example, enter "demo". When the static library is finally generated, a "lib" prefix and a ".a" suffix are added to generate "libdemo.a".

    **Figure 2**  Entering the static library name<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327223373_fig484053232210"></a>  
    ![](figures/输入静态库名字.png "Entering the static library name")

3.  Select the folders or files that contain the source files of the static library from the current project files. Multiple selection is supported. The tool automatically filters out .c files. After the selection, click Finish.

    **Figure 3**  Selecting the source files for generating the static library<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327223373_fig296914348228"></a>  
    ![](figures/选择要生成静态库的源文件.png "Selecting the source files for generating the static library")

4.  Select the header file paths required for compiling static dependencies from the current project files. Folders or files can be selected, and multiple selection is supported. The tool automatically filters out .h files. After the selection, click Finish.

    **Figure 4**  Selecting the header file paths for generating the static library<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327223373_fig875163710220"></a>  
    ![](figures/选择要生成静态库的头文件路径.png "Selecting the header file paths for generating the static library")

5.  Click "![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002439200448.png)" to compile. After a successful compilation, the static library file is generated in "out\\libs".

    **Figure 5**  Generation path of the static library<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327223373_fig2079334113225"></a>  
    ![](figures/静态库的生成路径.png "Generation path of the static library")

## Using a Static Library in Compilation<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378877460"></a>

**Using the Static Library Files in the Project<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327183621_section18863661645"></a>**

1.  Move the .a files corresponding to the custom static library generated in the "out\\libs" path to the "thirdparty\\sysroot\\lib" path.

    >![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
    >Do not place .a files in the "out\\libs" directory; otherwise, they will be cleared during clean.

2.  Move the .h files corresponding to the static library to the "thirdparty\\sysroot\\include" path.
3.  Include the corresponding header files in the project, and you can call the interfaces in the custom static library.

**Using Static Library Files Outside the Project<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327183621_section1866313180420"></a>**

1.  In the External Static Library Path, select the path of the .a file of the referenced external static library. In External Static Library Dependencies, select the directory of the header files on which the corresponding static library depends.

    **Figure 1**  External static library configuration items<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327183621_fig57631244152214"></a>  
    ![](figures/外部静态库配置项.png "External static library configuration items")

2.  After the selection, click Rebuild to compile, and include the header files on which the referenced static library depends in the project.

# Package Burning<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412436873"></a>

The burning function supports only serial port burning.




## Connecting the Burning Serial Cable<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717612"></a>

Software image burning uses the serial port communication protocol. The computer running the HiSpark Studio for VS Code plugin and the target board need to be connected with a serial cable. Common serial cables include standard serial cables and USB-to-serial cables. If you use a USB-to-serial cable, you need to install the USB-to-serial driver in advance.

**Figure 1**  Serial cable connection for burning<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327223389_fig8881486226"></a>  
![](figures/烧录串口连接示意图.png "Serial cable connection for burning")

## Configuring Project Burning Options<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412317029"></a>

1.  Set up the hardware environment properly.

    Connect the computer and the development board to be burned with a serial cable.

2.  <a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327183641_li207851059104511"></a>Determine the serial port number of the connected port.

    Open Device Manager on the computer, and view and record the serial port number corresponding to the serial cable.

    **Figure 1**  Serial port selection<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327183641_fig55913473219"></a>  
    ![](figures/串口选择.png "Serial port selection")

    >![](public_sys-resources/icon-notice.gif) **Notice:** 
    >If you burn through a USB-to-serial cable, install the USB-to-serial driver.

3.  Enter the project configuration interface.

    After opening the project to be burned, click the "![](figures/config-0.png)" button of the project configuration to enter the project configuration interface.

    **Figure 2**  Project configuration entry<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327183641_fig337113913219"></a>  
    ![](figures/工程配置入口.png "Project configuration entry")

4.  Click "Transmission Mode" under "Program Loading". The default is "serial" serial port transmission. In "Bin File", the Bin file to be burned is selected by default. Select the port number as described in [Step 2](#zh-cn_topic_0000002327183641_li207851059104511). The default baud rate is 115200.

    **Figure 3**  Serial port burning configuration<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327183641_fig99981772213"></a>  
    ![](figures/串口烧录配置.png "Serial port burning configuration")

    >![](public_sys-resources/icon-notice.gif) **Notice:** 
    >When FBB series chips use the "serial" burning mode, because it is a USB-to-serial connection, differences in hardware devices may limit the burning baud rate supported by the chips. To support a higher baud rate, the board needs to be modified. The burning baud rate of BRANDY series chips is limited to no more than 500000 by default, that of BS2X series chips is limited to no more than 2000000 by default, and that of 3322 is limited to no more than 750000 by default.

5.  Click the burn button  ![](figures/shaolu.png) in the toolbar to start burning.

    **Figure 4**  Burn button entry<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327183641_fig1518855418224"></a>  
    ![](figures/烧录按钮入口.png "Burn button entry")

6.  Reset the device as prompted. After a successful burning, the terminal window output is as shown in [Figure 5](#zh-cn_topic_0000002327183641_fig9672125742319).

    **Figure 5**  Burning successful<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327183641_fig9672125742319"></a>  
    ![](figures/烧录成功.png "Burning successful")

## Burning Configuration<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378877464"></a>

This section mainly introduces how to selectively burn bin files. If the compilation succeeds before using this function, start directly from [1](#zh-cn_topic_0000002293383702_p913mcpsimp) in this section. If the project has not been compiled, see the "[Compilation Results](编译结果.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717608)" section to compile it first, and then perform the operations according to the steps in this section.

1.  <a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293383702_p913mcpsimp"></a>Click the "Burning Configuration"  ![](figures/sp.png)  button in the toolbar to enter the burning configuration interface.

    **Figure 1**  Burning configuration button and interface<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293383702_fig18205858181719"></a>  
    
    ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002340660566.png)

2.  The FBB partition file is the packaged .fwpkg file by default, or you can click the "Browse" button to select a packaged .fwpkg file from local files. After the selection, the burning tool automatically lists the bin contents contained in the .fwpkg file.

    **Figure 2**  FBB burning configuration partition file path<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293383702_fig7273738121914"></a>  
    
    ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002374785221.png)

3.  Check the .bin files to be burned. All .bin files are checked by default. The editing state of rows containing the partition name attribute of loader.bin or ssb.bin in the table cannot be modified. Other .bin files can be checked or unchecked as needed for burning.
4.  Before burning, you need to configure the transmission mode and other parameter information. For details, see the "[Flasher Configuration](烧录器配置.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717604)" section.
5.  Click the "Burn" button and restart the development board as prompted to start burning.

    **Figure 3**  Burning<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293383702_fig7508701238"></a>  
    
    ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002374672533.png)

6.  After burning starts, a burning progress bar is displayed below the partition file for you to view the burning progress. After burning starts, the related command information is printed in the terminal. Finally, after a successful burning, the word "successfully" is displayed on the interface.

    **Figure 4**  Burning progress<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293383702_fig19626111222410"></a>  
    
    ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002374674917.png)

    **Figure 5**  Burning start printing<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293383702_fig377133292415"></a>  
    
    ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002374810285.png)

    **Figure 6**  Burning start printing<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293383702_fig17310339133719"></a>  
    ![](figures/烧写开始打印.png "Burning start printing")

# Stack Analysis and Image Analysis<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412436881"></a>

The HiSpark Studio for VS Code plugin integrates the Stack Analysis tool and the Image Analysis tool to analyze problems such as insufficient memory and memory overflow during development, helping developers analyze and locate problems more accurately.

-   The Stack Analysis tool is based on static binary analysis. It provides task stack overhead estimates and function call relationship diagrams, providing relatively accurate static memory analysis data references for development scenarios such as stack memory usage, analysis, optimization, and problem location.
-   The Image Analysis tool analyzes the memory usage of the .elf file built from the project, helping developers quickly evaluate the usage of memory segments and symbol tables.



## Stack Analysis<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717620"></a>

**Function Introduction<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293224062_section148321529151417"></a>**

The Stack Analysis tool is based on static binary analysis. It provides task stack overhead estimates and function call relationship diagrams, providing relatively accurate static memory analysis data references for development scenarios such as stack memory usage, analysis, optimization, and problem location.

**Function Entry<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293224062_section17325123961615"></a>**

After creating a project and compiling it successfully, click the "![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002293384078.png)" button in the toolbar to perform stack analysis.

**Stack Analysis Function Page<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293224062_section125419179175"></a>**

The stack analysis results are displayed as a function list and call relationships. As shown in [Figure 1](#zh-cn_topic_0000002293224062_fig4596122219257), the function list page displays the name, internal stack overhead, and location information of each function. The unit of internal stack overhead is Byte. Keyword search and sorting are supported.

**Figure 1**  Function list page<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293224062_fig4596122219257"></a>  
![](figures/功能列表页面.png "Function list page")

The call relationship interface is shown in [Figure 2](#zh-cn_topic_0000002293224062_fig1384164712519). It displays the call relationships of each function, including the function name, call depth, maximum stack overhead, and internal stack overhead. Keyword search and sorting are supported.

**Figure 2**  Call graph page<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293224062_fig1384164712519"></a>  
![](figures/调用图页面.png "Call graph page")

**Statistics Item Description<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293224062_section618mcpsimp"></a>**

-   Maximum overhead: The product of the maximum stack overhead among all subfunctions of the current function and the number of loop calls, plus its own overhead.

    Calculation formula: max(own stack overhead of subfunction 1, own stack overhead of subfunction 2, own stack overhead of subfunction 3, ...) × number of loop calls + own stack overhead of the function

-   Local overhead: The own stack overhead of the current function.
-   Depth: For each additional level of subfunction of the current function, the depth increases by one level.

## Image Analysis<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412317033"></a>

**Function Introduction<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327223425_section538803119292"></a>**

The Image Analysis tool analyzes the .elf file and graphically displays the usage of RAM and ROM.

**Function Entry<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327223425_section1582295611297"></a>**

After creating a project and compiling it successfully, click the "![](figures/jing.png)" button in the toolbar.

**Function Interface<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327223425_section1533673619307"></a>**

The memory region page (as shown in [Figure 1](#zh-cn_topic_0000002327223425_fig1787391122717)) evaluates and analyzes the detailed memory usage of the project. For example, for WS63, the displayed memory regions include RAM, SRAM, ITCM, and others. The displayed information includes the name, start memory address, end memory address, total size, free size, used size, and usage ratio of each memory region. Keyword search and sorting are supported, as shown in [Figure 1](#zh-cn_topic_0000002327223425_fig1787391122717).

**Figure 1**  Memory region page<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327223425_fig1787391122717"></a>  
![](figures/内存区域页面.png "Memory region page")

The memory details page (as shown in [Figure 2](#zh-cn_topic_0000002327223425_fig15591937172713)) displays the details of the memory sections contained in each memory region and the symbols contained in each memory section. For example, FLASH contains memory sections such as .text, .entry, and .data, and each memory section contains the program symbols allocated to it. Keyword search and sorting are supported.

The information displayed in each row includes the runtime address VMA (Virtual Memory Address, indicating the memory address where the program is loaded), the load address LMA (Load Memory Address, indicating the memory address where the program runs), and the size of the memory section/symbol.

**Figure 2**  Memory details page<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327223425_fig15591937172713"></a>  
![](figures/内存详细信息页面.png "Memory details page")

The file size page (as shown in [Figure 3](#zh-cn_topic_0000002327223425_fig9261641289)) displays which memory space each linked .o file occupies and the size of the occupied space. Keyword search and sorting are supported.

**Figure 3**  File size page<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327223425_fig9261641289"></a>  
![](figures/文件大小页面.png "File size page")

The module size page (as shown in [Figure 4](#zh-cn_topic_0000002327223425_fig1367222122813)) displays the hierarchy of modules and components and the memory usage of different modules. Keyword search and sorting are supported.

**Figure 4**  Module size page<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327223425_fig1367222122813"></a>  
![](figures/模块大小页面.png "Module size page")

The folder size page (as shown in [Figure 5](#zh-cn_topic_0000002327223425_fig1075993892817)) displays the memory usage of modules under different folders. Keyword search and sorting are supported, and export to Excel is supported.

**Figure 5**  Folder size<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327223425_fig1075993892817"></a>  
![](figures/文件夹大小.png "Folder size")

# Project Debugging<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378877472"></a>




## Debug Configuration Options<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412436885"></a>

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>The JlinkGDBServerCL driver in the debug configuration options needs to be downloaded from the J-Link official website. The official website address is: [https://www.segger.com/products/debug-probes/j-link/models/j-link-base/](https://www.segger.com/products/debug-probes/j-link/models/j-link-base/)

1.  Select the project to debug: In the main interface of the HiSpark Studio for VS Code plugin, select the project to debug and open "Project Configuration".
2.  Modify the debug options and select the corresponding debugger.

    **Figure 1**  Modifying debug options<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293383734_fig4891141904416"></a>  
    ![](figures/调试选项修改.png "Modifying debug options")

## Starting Debugging<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717632"></a>

1.  Click the debug button "![](figures/34.png)" in the IDE toolbar, and select the required debug mode in the pop-up at the top.

    **Figure 1**  Debug mode selection<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293224078_fig0277550163411"></a>  
    ![](figures/调试模式选择.png "Debug mode selection")

    -   GDB Launch (Acore): The A core restarts, the CPU pauses, and the PC pointer is set to run the program from the beginning (a virtual breakpoint is set at the beginning of the A core).
    -   GDB Attach (Acore): While the A core is running, the CPU pauses, and the program stops directly at the CPU Halt.
    -   GDB Launch (Pcore): The P core restarts, the CPU pauses, and the PC pointer is set to run the program from the beginning (a virtual breakpoint is set at the beginning of the P core).
    -   GDB Attach (Pcore): While the P core is running, the CPU pauses, and the program stops directly at the CPU Halt.

2.  Debugging starts successfully. An example of a successful debugging start is shown in the figure below. If the following prompt message and the toolbar debug icon appear, it indicates that debugging has been started successfully.

    **Figure 2**  Prompt information, debug icons, and debug interface after a successful debugging start<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293224078_fig1214081317358"></a>  
    ![](figures/调试成功后提示信息-调试图标及调试界面.png "Prompt information, debug icons, and debug interface after a successful debugging start")

    ![](figures/3534.png)

    ![](figures/777.png)

## Common Debugging Functions<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412317037"></a>


### Debugging Page<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378877476"></a>

The debugging work interface is shown in [Figure 1](#zh-cn_topic_0000002327183693_fig132922116210), which mainly consists of the following three parts:

① Debugging sidebar

② Debugging function area

③ Debugging console

**Figure 1**  Debugging work interface<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327183693_fig132922116210"></a>  
![](figures/调试工作界面.png "Debugging work interface")




#### Debugging Sidebar<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412436889"></a>

The debugging sidebar provides common debugging functions, including variables, watch, call stack, breakpoints, and others.

#### Debugging Function Area<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717640"></a>

After debugging starts, when the code execution reaches a set breakpoint, the program pauses. You can debug the code using the buttons in the debugging function area.

**Figure 1**  Debug icons<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293224090_fig1952516244357"></a>  
![](figures/调试图标.png "Debug icons")

-   ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002327223797.png): Continue ("F5"). When the program execution reaches a breakpoint, it stops. Click this button to continue the program execution.
-   ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002293384106.png): Step Over ("F10"). During step debugging, it moves directly to the next line (if the function contains subfunctions, it does not step into the subfunctions, but executes the entire subfunction as one step).
-   ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002293224438.png): Step Into ("F11"). During step debugging, when a subfunction is encountered, it enters the subfunction and continues step-by-step execution.
-   ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002327223801.png): Step Out ("Shift+F11"). When step debugging executes into a subfunction, clicking Step Out executes the remaining part of the subfunction and returns to the upper-level function.
-   ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002327184057.png): Restart Debugging ("Ctrl+Shift+F5"), to restart debugging.
-   ![](figures/tt7.png): Stop Debugging ("Shift+F5"), to stop the debugging task and disconnect.

#### Debugging Console<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412317041"></a>

The debugging console is used to output printing information during debugging, and you can also enter commands to interact with the debugger.

-   View variables

    When the program pauses at a breakpoint, you can view the current values of variables on the variables interface.

    **Figure 1**  Viewing the current value of a variable<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327223477_fig122981731183513"></a>  
    ![](figures/查看变量当前值.png "Viewing the current value of a variable")

    The following four variable types are supported:

    -   Local variables
    -   Global variables (may be optimized by the compiler. You can use the volatile keyword to avoid this problem.)
    -   Static variables (may be optimized by the compiler. You can use the volatile keyword to avoid this problem.)
    -   Registers

-   Watch function

    During debugging, you can use "Watch" to view the values of variables (including local, global, and static variables) and specific addresses to determine whether the program running result is correct.

    **Figure 2**  Watch function<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327223477_fig154504387355"></a>  
    ![](figures/监视功能.png "Watch function")

-   View call stack

    During debugging, you can view the call stack to analyze the call relationships among the subprograms called by the main program, as shown in the following figure.

    **Figure 3**  Call stack function<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327223477_fig102391744183516"></a>  
    ![](figures/调用堆栈功能.png "Call stack function")

    **----End**

# Serial Port Console Tool<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378877480"></a>

This section mainly introduces the monitor tool for serial port operations in the HiSpark Studio for VS Code plugin. Its main functions include displaying the serial port list, connecting to a serial port, disconnecting a serial port, receiving serial port messages, sending messages to a serial port, clearing the serial port output area, and enabling\\disabling automatic screen scrolling.

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>The serial port tool uses "\\r\\n" as the separator for each line. Therefore, each line of the printed string must end with "\\r\\n"; otherwise, the printing at the end of the program may be lost.







## Opening the Monitor<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412436893"></a>

After running the HiSpark Studio for VS Code plugin, open the VS Code terminal, find the terminal area, and switch to the "Monitor" tab, as shown in [Figure 1](#zh-cn_topic_0000002293224166_fig17771812125220).

**Figure 1**  Switching to the "Monitor" tab<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293224166_fig17771812125220"></a>  
![](figures/切换到-监视器-选项卡.png "Switching to the Monitor tab")

**Figure 2**  Monitor interface introduction<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293224166_fig14318546512"></a>  

![](figures/terminal.png)

-   ①: Serial port configuration area

    Port: Displays the serial port devices connected to the current computer. Click the "![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002327184165.png)" button to refresh the serial port list.

    Baud rate: Select the serial port baud rate. Range: 300 to 250000.

    Line ending: When sending messages to the serial port, the tool automatically appends characters based on this option.

    -   CRLF represents "\\r\\n".
    -   CR represents "\\r".
    -   LF represents "\\n".

-   ②: Function button area

    ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002293384214.png): Connect serial port button. After the serial port is connected, the button state changes to ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002293224546.png). Clicking this button disconnects the serial port.

    ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002327223909.png): Timestamp button. When enabled, a timestamp is added before each line of output. If the button is off, no timestamp is displayed, as shown in [Figure 3](#zh-cn_topic_0000002293224166_fig1040102615572).

    **Figure 3**  Example of the timestamp setting effect<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293224166_fig1040102615572"></a>  
    ![](figures/时间戳设置效果示例.png "Example of the timestamp setting effect")

    ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002293384218.png): Hide input box. The interfaces for hiding/showing the input box are shown in [Figure 4](#zh-cn_topic_0000002293224166_fig209583373583) and [Figure 5](#zh-cn_topic_0000002293224166_fig17214483580).

    **Figure 4**  Showing the input box<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293224166_fig209583373583"></a>  
    ![](figures/显示输入框.png "Showing the input box")

    **Figure 5**  Hiding the input box<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293224166_fig17214483580"></a>  
    ![](figures/隐藏输入框.png "Hiding the input box")

    ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002327184173.png): Enable/disable automatic screen scrolling.

    ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002293384226.png): Clear the output area.

    ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002293224562.png): Maximize the panel.

    ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002327223921.png): Close the panel

-   ③: Output area.
-   ④: Input area.
-   ⑤: Send message button. Click the button or press Enter to send the information in the input area to the serial port. The default encoding is UTF-8.

## Connecting to a Serial Port<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717648"></a>

Click "![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002327184177.png)" to connect to the serial port. Before connecting, the input area is inactive by default (cannot be entered or clicked). After connecting, the input area becomes active, and the monitoring mode option in the serial port configuration area becomes inactive.

**Figure 1**  Starting the monitoring function<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327223529_fig58721954163517"></a>  
![](figures/开始监视功能.png "Starting the monitoring function")

## Viewing Messages<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412317045"></a>

You can view the messages sent by the serial port in the output area.

**Figure 1**  Viewing serial port messages<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327183793_fig15659205953510"></a>  
![](figures/查看串口消息.png "Viewing serial port messages")

## Sending Messages<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378877488"></a>

Enter a message in the input area below, and click the send button or press the Enter key on the keyboard to send the message.

**Figure 1**  Sending a message<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293383846_fig1571552215364"></a>  
![](figures/发送消息.png "Sending a message")

## Disconnecting<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412436897"></a>

Click the "![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002327184181.png)" button to disconnect the serial port.

**Figure 1**  Stopping monitoring<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293224182_fig4728173953619"></a>  
![](figures/停止监视.png "Stopping monitoring")

## Using Extension Tools<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717652"></a>

Extension tools are mainly used to add some commonly used commands. After adding, you can click a name to send the command. The extension tools include some common commands by default. If you do not need these commands, you can clear the table and manually add the required commands, or add them by importing an Excel table. In addition, the extension tools also provide a loop sending function, which will be described in detail in the following sections.

-   Open the extension tools. Click the extension button, and the extension page expands on the right side.

    **Figure 1**  Extension tools<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327223541_fig1786951684210"></a>  
    ![](figures/扩展工具.png "Extension tools")

    Click this button to maximize the panel.

    **Figure 2**  Maximize button of the monitor panel<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327223541_fig11226132364210"></a>  
    ![](figures/监视器面板最大化按钮.png "Maximize button of the monitor panel")

-   Click the clear list button to clear the list.

    **Figure 3**  Clear list function<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327223541_fig6150152913426"></a>  
    ![](figures/清空列表功能.png "Clear list function")

    If you need to restore the cleared factory table, you can import the "rawData.xlsx" table under "C:\\Users\\username\\.vscode\\extensions\\hispark.hisparkstudio-x.x.x\\dist\\resources\\terminal\\resources\\excelFile" in the installation directory.

-   Manually add a command line. Click the "Add a Row" button.

    **Figure 4**  Adding a row function<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327223541_fig1455312345424"></a>  
    ![](figures/新增一行功能.png "Adding a row function")

    Data addition rules:

    Data format: Drop-down box mode. The drop-down options are utf8, bin, and hex.

    Command: The command string sent to the serial port.

    Name: The string used to identify the command function. Click this button to immediately send the command to the serial port (provided that the tool is in the listening state).

    Order: When using the loop sending function, the loop sending is performed only when the order is greater than 0. If the order of multiple commands is greater than 0 and the values are the same, the commands are sent from top to bottom.

    Delayed sending: Send once first, and then delay. Unit: ms.

    Operation: There are two options, Edit and Delete. Click Edit to modify the command line, and click Delete to delete the command line.

-   Add commands in batches.
    1.  Download the import template. The import template is automatically saved to "C:\\Users\\username\\.vscode\\extensions\\hispark.hisparkstudio-x.x.x\\resources\\terminal\\resources\\excelFile\\template.xlsx".

        **Figure 5**  Downloading the import template function<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327223541_fig131081446114215"></a>  
        ![](figures/下载导入模板功能.png "Downloading the import template function")

        The import template shows the columns to be imported and the rules for each column.

        **Figure 6**  Import template<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327223541_fig4205125119425"></a>  
        ![](figures/导入模板.png "Import template")

    2.  Fill in the import template.

        **Figure 7**  Filling in the import template data<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327223541_fig249315718422"></a>  
        ![](figures/导入模板数据填写.png "Filling in the import template data")

    3.  Import the "Import Template" file.

        **Figure 8**  Importing template data<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327223541_fig11424622434"></a>  
        ![](figures/导入模板数据.png "Importing template data")

        ![](figures/Snipaste_2025-05-26_19-25-41.png)

    4.  View the imported data.

        **Figure 9**  Viewing the imported data<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327223541_fig18481158134314"></a>  
        ![](figures/查看导入数据.png "Viewing the imported data")

        ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002327184197.png)

    5.  View the import result table. The Import Result column displays the reasons for the failed imports.

        **Figure 10**  Viewing the import results<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327223541_fig1017011484311"></a>  
        ![](figures/查看导入结果.png "Viewing the import results")

-   Loop sending.
    1.  Set the order and delay.

        **Figure 11**  Setting the order and delay function<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327223541_fig6466102611434"></a>  
        ![](figures/设置顺序和延时功能.png "Setting the order and delay function")

        The order in which the commands in the above table are executed:

        First, send the "Start Bluetooth" command and delay for 1000ms. Then, send the "Keyboard Mode" command and delay for 1000ms. Finally, send the "Mouse Mode" command and delay for 1000ms. End. This is one cycle of loop sending, and the loop sending keeps repeating this cycle.

        The "Modify Bluetooth Address" command is not sent because its order is not greater than 0.

    2.  Enable serial port listening.

        **Figure 12**  Serial port listening function<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327223541_fig5219203116435"></a>  
        ![](figures/串口监听功能.png "Serial port listening function")

    3.  Enable the loop sending switch (the sent commands carry "carriage return and line feed" by default). After it is enabled, the sent commands can be seen in the output column on the left.

        **Figure 13**  Loop sending function<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327223541_fig734614365431"></a>  
        ![](figures/循环发送功能.png "Loop sending function")

        Red text indicates that the command is being sent or the delay is in progress.

# Kconfig Configuration<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412317049"></a>

This function is mainly used to control the compilation and building of the project, and supports managing the compilation configuration through a graphical interface.

After opening the project, click the system configuration "![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002327291945.png)" button in the toolbar, as shown in [Figure 1](#zh-cn_topic_0000002327183797_fig1334674314438).

**Figure 1**  System configuration entry<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327183797_fig1334674314438"></a>  
![](figures/系统配置入口.png "System configuration entry")

The system configuration interface is shown in [Figure 2](#zh-cn_topic_0000002327183797_fig11641828725).

**Figure 2**  System configuration interface<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327183797_fig11641828725"></a>  
![](figures/系统配置界面.png "System configuration interface")


## Button Functions<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378877492"></a>

The button functions on the system configuration interface are as follows:

① save: The configuration file is saved to \`$\{menu\_config\_build\_target\}\` by default.

② save as: Customize the save path. The default configuration file name is \`$\{menu\_config\_build\_target\}\`.config.

③ save\(minimal\): Customize the save path and the configuration file name, and save only the modified configuration items.

④ open: Customize the loading of a configuration file.

⑤ jump to: Search for configuration items.

⑥ show name: Display the column names (Option-Name).

⑦ show all: Display hidden configuration items.

⑧ Single-menu mode: Single menu mode.

# GUI Project Creation and Usage<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412436901"></a>

HiSpark Studio supports creating GUI projects and using the GUI simulator. After creating a Brandy project as described in "[3.1 Creating a Project](新建工程.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412436861)", click the "Create GUI Application" icon under the toolbar menu to create a GUI project in the Brandy project.

**Figure 1**  Creating a GUI project<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739733_fig1874373411538"></a>  
![](figures/创建GUI工程.png "Creating a GUI project")

-   Pixel value: The default 454×454 corresponds to the generated canvas size. When the shape is circle, the resolution is diameter×diameter. When the shape is rectangle, the resolution is length×width. The pixel value can be customized within the range of 1 to 600.
-   Shape: Divided into circle and rectangle. If you do not actively select one, the default is circle.





## GUI Interface Introduction<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717656"></a>



### GUI Drag-and-Drop Interface Introduction<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412317053"></a>

After the GUI project is created, a GUI file named after the project is created in the "application/wearable/nativeapp/nativeui/application name" directory of the project. When opened, it presents the shape of a watch face, and the watch face content can be customized.

Currently supported components include Button, Image, Progress, List, Label, ToggleButton, CheckBox, RadioButton, LabelButton, ScrollView, CircleProgress, ArcLabel, SwipeView, TextureMapper, Slider, Picker, TimePicker, Chart, EditText, ImageAnimator, DigitalClock, Qrcode, AnalogClock, SweepClock, Coverflow, Barcode, CardPage, CrossView, MapView, RollerView, HexagonsList, IcosahedronView, CanvasExt, TransformList, SlipflowView, TransformGroup, Coverflow2, ParticleView, and the Root component.

**Figure 1**  GUI drag-and-drop interface<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619865_fig1973444016553"></a>  

![](figures/gui8.png)

-   FileTree: Page tree. You can add or delete pages, modify page titles, and set the main page.
-   UI Control: UI component bar. You can select the corresponding component and drag it to the canvas (Canvas) to add the component.
-   Component Tree: Component tree. In the low-code development interface, developers can intuitively view the hierarchical structure and summary information of components. By selecting a component in the component tree (the corresponding component in the canvas is selected simultaneously), developers can quickly locate the component in the canvas. Clicking the "![](figures/Y.png)" after the component can hide/show the corresponding component.
-   Panel: Function panel, including the subcomponent switching used by the CrossView and Coverflow2 components, as well as common operations such as zooming the canvas in/out, aligning components left, vertically centered, right, top, horizontally centered, and bottom, layer switching, undo, showing/hiding the virtual borders of components, and generating C++ code with one click.
-   Canvas: Canvas. Developers can perform visual operations such as dragging and stretching components in this area to build the UI layout.
-   Attributes & Styles: Attribute and style bar. After selecting the corresponding component in the canvas, you can configure the attributes and styles of the component in the attribute and style bar on the right.
    -   General: Corresponding to the icon ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002366100070.png), used to set common styles such as Width, Height, Background, Position, and Display.
    -   Feature: Corresponding to the icon ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399619989.png), used to set the specific styles of the component, such as the FontSize style describing the text size of the Text component.
    -   Events: Corresponding to the icon ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002365940174.png), used to bind related events to the component and set the callback functions of the bound events.

### Component Introduction<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378877496"></a>













































#### Copying, Pasting, and Deleting a Component<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412436905"></a>

Take the Button component as an example. Perform the operations on the premise that a Button component has been dragged into the canvas:

-   Deleting a component

    A component can be deleted in two ways:

    -   Right-click the Button component in the canvas to display the operation bar, and click Delete in the popped-up operation bar.
    -   Select the Button component and press the Del key on the keyboard to delete it.

    **Figure 1**  Deleting a component<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739737_fig1276601114010"></a>  
    ![](figures/组件删除.png "Deleting a component")

-   Copying a component

    A component can be copied in two ways:

    -   Right-click the Button component in the canvas to display the operation bar, click Copy in the popped-up operation bar, then right-click again and select Paste to implement copy and paste.
    -   Select the Button component, and use Ctrl+C and Ctrl+V on the keyboard to implement copy and paste.

    **Figure 2**  Copying a component<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739737_fig1175415591409"></a>  
    ![](figures/组件复制.png "Copying a component")

    **Figure 3**  Pasting a component<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739737_fig14301013164114"></a>  
    ![](figures/组件粘贴.png "Pasting a component")

#### Common Attributes of a Component<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717660"></a>

Take the Button component as an example. Select the Button component in the component bar, drag it to the central canvas area, and release the mouse to add a Button component. (The component is generated at the position of coordinate (100, 100) on the canvas.)

**Figure 1**  Dragging a component<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099954_fig10331115148"></a>  
![](figures/拖拽组件.png "Dragging a component")

The following operations need to be performed on the premise that a Button component has been dragged into the canvas.

-   Select the Button component in the canvas, and modify the ID of the Button component in the expanded General bar through the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399739865.png) style icon (General) in the attribute and style bar on the right. The ID is not the variable name used when generating code, so multiple components can be set to the same ID.

    **Figure 2**  Common attribute configuration: Id<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099954_fig1333343519513"></a>  
    ![](figures/通用属性配置-Id.png "Common attribute configuration: Id")

-   Select the Button component in the canvas, and modify the width, height, and position of the Button component in the expanded General bar through the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399620009.png) style icon (General) in the attribute and style bar on the right. In addition, you can also zoom in/out the Button component and change its position by dragging.

    **Figure 3**  Common attribute configuration<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099954_fig9966191695113"></a>  
    ![](figures/通用属性的配置.png "Common attribute configuration")

-   Select the Button component, click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399739869.png) style icon (General) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and modify the BackgroundColor attribute of the Button component in the expanded General bar to change the background color.

    **Figure 4**  Modifying the background color<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099954_fig759121615415"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改背景颜色.png "Modifying the background color")

-   Select the Button component in the canvas, and modify the FullScreenState and GaussianBlur attributes in the GaussOption box of the Button component in the expanded General bar through the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399620013.png) style icon (General) in the attribute and style bar on the right, to change the Gaussian blur of the component. This is performed on the premise that two components are dragged at the same time. When FullScreenState is false, the Gaussian blur setting does not take effect. When FullScreenState is true, the Gaussian blur setting takes effect. The GaussianBlur attribute controls the blur degree of the Gaussian blur. For details, see "[Gaussian Blur Attribute](高斯模糊属性.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378877540)".

    **Figure 5**  Gaussian blur<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099954_fig48601252103118"></a>  
    ![](figures/高斯模糊.png "Gaussian blur")

-   Select the Button component in the canvas, and modify the Width, Color, and Radius attributes in the Border box of the Button component in the expanded General bar through the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399739877.png) style icon (General) in the attribute and style bar on the right, to change the border style.

    **Figure 6**  Modifying the component border style<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099954_fig421010341275"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改组件边框样式.png "Modifying the component border style")

-   Select the Button component, click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399620017.png) style icon (General) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and modify the MarginTop and MarginLeft attributes in the Margin box of the Button component in the expanded General bar to adjust the position of the component relative to the watch face.

    **Figure 7**  Modifying the component position relative to the window<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099954_fig19389527155313"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改组件相对窗口位置.png "Modifying the component position relative to the window")

-   Select the Button component, click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399739897.png) style icon (General) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and modify Padding in the expanded General bar to change the width of the padding part of the component in the four directions.

    **Figure 8**  Component position relative to the border<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099954_fig88161839549"></a>  
    ![](figures/组件相对边框位置.png "Component position relative to the border")

#### Button Component<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412317057"></a>

For the usage of common attributes, see "[Common Attributes of a Component](组件的共有属性.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717660)".

-   Select the Button component in the canvas, and modify the DefaultImgSrc attribute of the Button component in the expanded Feature bar through the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399620053.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right, to load an image.

    **Figure 1**  DefaultImgSrc attribute configuration<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619869_fig1930105513132"></a>  
    ![](figures/DefaultImgSrc属性配置.png "DefaultImgSrc attribute configuration")

-   Select the Button component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399739953.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and modify the ImageX and ImageY attributes of the Button component in the expanded Feature bar to adjust the image position (you need to set the DefaultImgSrc attribute before modification).

    **Figure 2**  ImageX/ImageY attribute configuration<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619869_fig11684412161613"></a>  
    ![](figures/ImageX-ImageY属性配置.png "ImageX-ImageY attribute configuration")

-   Select the Button component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399620097.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and modify the ImageOpacity attribute of the Button component in the expanded Feature bar to adjust the image transparency (you need to set DefaultImgSrc).

    **Figure 3**  ImageOpacity attribute configuration<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619869_fig11511623928"></a>  
    ![](figures/ImageOpacity属性配置.png "ImageOpacity attribute configuration")

-   Select the Button component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399739961.png) style icon (Events) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and configure the callback events in the expanded Events bar.

    The events supported by the Button component include: OnClick, OnLongPress, OnPress, OnCancel, and OnRelease.

    **Figure 4**  Callback event configuration<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619869_fig141561846154620"></a>  
    ![](figures/回调事件配置.png "Callback event configuration")

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>-   The MarginBottom and MarginRight attributes under the General page have no interface rendering effect.
>-   The TriggeredImgSrc, TriggeredBackgroundColor, and TriggeredBorderColor attributes under the Feature page have no interface rendering effect. You can view the rendering effect in the simulator.
>-   DefaultImgSrc and TriggeredImgSrc do not support Chinese paths.

#### Image Component<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378877500"></a>

For the usage of common attributes, see the "[Common Attributes of a Component](组件的共有属性.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717660)" section.

-   Select the Image component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399620105.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and modify the ImagePath attribute of the Image component in the expanded Feature bar to load an image.

    If the same image is selected again after an image has already been selected, or if the window is closed or Cancel is clicked while browsing files, the interface rendering effect will not change. To clear the image, delete the text in the input box.

    **Figure 1**  Specific attribute configuration: ImagePath<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002365940058_fig1383616220353"></a>  
    ![](figures/特有属性配置-ImagePath.png "Specific attribute configuration - ImagePath")

    >![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
    >ImagePath does not support Chinese paths.
    >The imported Bin file must be a Bin converted from an image; otherwise, the simulator will behave abnormally.

-   Select the Image component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399739965.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and modify the ImageOpacity attribute of the Image component in the expanded Feature bar to adjust the image transparency.

    **Figure 2**  Specific attribute configuration: ImageOpacity<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002365940058_fig19953128113813"></a>  
    ![](figures/特有属性配置-ImageOpacity.png "Specific attribute configuration - ImageOpacity")

-   Select the Image component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399620109.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and modify the AutoEnable attribute of the Image component in the expanded Feature bar.

    -   When the AutoEnable attribute is set to true, the component is limited to the size of the image itself, and the display effect of the component cannot be modified by dragging the component or directly modifying the Width/Height values.
    -   When the AutoEnable attribute is set to false, the image is displayed according to the component size. Different ImageResizeMode attribute values present different display effects.

    **Figure 3**  Specific attribute configuration: AutoEnable<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002365940058_fig32031181480"></a>  
    ![](figures/特有属性配置-AutoEnable.png "Specific attribute configuration - AutoEnable")

-   Select the Image component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399739969.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and modify the ImageResizeMode attribute of the Image component in the expanded Feature bar.

    -   When the AutoEnable attribute is set to true, the ImageResizeMode attribute does not take effect.
    -   When the AutoEnable attribute is set to false, ImageResizeMode takes effect, corresponding to different display effects.

    The effect examples corresponding to the 6 modes are as follows (the original size of the image is 80×80):

    -   ImageResizeMode - None

        **Figure 4**  Image tiling (component size in the figure is 200×200)<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002365940058_fig137143716018"></a>  
        ![](figures/图片平铺（图中组件大小为200-200）.png "Image tiling (component size in the figure is 200×200)")

    -   ImageResizeMode - Cover

        **Figure 5**  Image covering the component 1 (component size in the figure is 100×200)<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002365940058_fig203183291602"></a>  
        ![](figures/图片覆盖组件1（图中组件大小为100-200）.png "Image covering the component 1 (component size in the figure is 100×200)")

        **Figure 6**  Image covering the component 2 (component size in the figure is 200×100)<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002365940058_fig119815431203"></a>  
        ![](figures/图片覆盖组件2（图中组件大小为200-100）.png "Image covering the component 2 (component size in the figure is 200×100)")

    -   ImageResizeMode - Contain

        **Figure 7**  Image contained in the component (component size in the figure is 100×200)<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002365940058_fig9776119513"></a>  
        ![](figures/图片被组件包含在内（图中组件大小为100-200）.png "Image contained in the component (component size in the figure is 100×200)")

        **Figure 8**  Image contained in the component (component size in the figure is 200×100)<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002365940058_fig1741815478118"></a>  
        ![](figures/图片被组件包含在内（图中组件大小为200-100）.png "Image contained in the component (component size in the figure is 200×100)")

    -   ImageResizeMode - Fill

        **Figure 9**  Image filling the component (component size in the figure is 200×100)<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002365940058_fig143226171166"></a>  
        ![](figures/图片填满组件（图中组件大小为200-100）.png "Image filling the component (component size in the figure is 200×100)")

    -   ImageResizeMode - Center

        **Figure 10**  Image at the center of the component (component size in the figure is 100×100)<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002365940058_fig78523241168"></a>  
        ![](figures/图片处在组件中心（图中组件大小为100-100）.png "Image at the center of the component (component size in the figure is 100×100)")

        >![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
        >When either the width or height of the component is 0, the image is hidden.

    -   ImageResizeMode - Scale Down

        **Figure 11**  When the component width and height are large, the image is displayed at its original size<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002365940058_fig1319195718418"></a>  
        ![](figures/组件宽高较大时-图片按原有尺寸显示.png "When the component width and height are large, the image is displayed at its original size")

        **Figure 12**  When the component width and height are small, the image is scaled down and contained in the component<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002365940058_fig89661415359"></a>  
        ![](figures/组件宽高较小时-图片会缩小并被组件包含在内.png "When the component width and height are small, the image is scaled down and contained in the component")

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>-   When the specific attribute ImagePath of the component is not set, the component is not displayed in the simulator.
>-   The MarginBottom and MarginRight attributes under the General page have no interface rendering effect. You can view the effect in the simulator.
>-   The BlurLevel and Algorithm attributes under the Feature page have no interface rendering effect. You can view the effect in the simulator.
>-   The Image component does not support configuring callback events.

#### Progress Component<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412436909"></a>

For the usage of common attributes, see the "[Common Attributes of a Component](组件的共有属性.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717660)" section.

-   Select the Progress component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399620153.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the foreground color of the Progress component in the ForegroundStyle window of the expanded Feature bar.

    **Figure 1**  Modifying the foreground color<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739741_fig117331435123510"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改前景色.png "Modifying the foreground color")

-   Select the Progress component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399740017.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the background color of the Progress component in the BackgroundStyle window of the expanded Feature bar.

    **Figure 2**  Modifying the background color<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739741_fig879142417363"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改背景色.png "Modifying the background color")

-   Select the Progress component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399620157.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the ProgressWidth and ProgressHeight attributes of the Progress component in the expanded Feature bar to change the width and height of the component.

    **Figure 3**  Modifying the component width and height<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739741_fig119251539922"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改组件宽高.png "Modifying the component width and height")

-   Select the Progress component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399740029.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the Direction attribute of the Progress component in the expanded Feature bar to change the direction of the progress bar.

    **Figure 4**  Modifying the direction of the progress bar<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739741_fig4795118183012"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改进度条方向.png "Modifying the direction of the progress bar")

-   Select the Progress component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399620269.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the Value attribute of the Progress component in the expanded Feature bar to change the progress.

    **Figure 5**  Modifying the progress<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739741_fig18362191916365"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改进度.png "Modifying the progress")

-   Select the Progress component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399740281.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. In the expanded Feature bar, modify the ForeImage attribute of the Progress component to add the image of the progress already passed and the BackImage attribute to add the overall image of the progress bar. The path cannot be empty and Chinese paths are not supported.

    **Figure 6**  Adding images<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739741_fig9386125012810"></a>  
    ![](figures/添加图片.png "Adding images")

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>-   The BackgroungColor, MarginBottom, and MarginRight attributes under the General page have no rendering effect for the time being.
>-   The sub-attributes BorderWidth, BorderColor, BorderRadius, LineWidth, LineHeight, LineColor, and ImageOpacity of ForegroundStyle and BackgroundStyle under the Feature page have no rendering effect for the time being.
>-   The Progress component does not support configuring callback events.

#### List Component<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717664"></a>

For the usage of common attributes, see the "[Common Attributes of a Component](组件的共有属性.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717660)" section.

-   Select the List component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399620549.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. In the expanded Feature bar, modify the itemHeight attribute to set the height of all items, modify the itemWidth attribute to set the width of all items, modify the imageWidth attribute to set the width of the images of all items, modify the imageHeight attribute to set the height of the images of all items, and modify the FontSize attribute to set the font size of all items. Click the plus sign on the right of the Item column to add an item, and modify the Text and ImagePath attributes of the item to add text and images.

    **Figure 1**  Adding an item<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099958_fig28061923305"></a>  
    ![](figures/添加子项.png "Adding an item")

-   Select the List component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399740433.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. In the expanded Feature bar, modify the OffsetX attribute of the List component to set the horizontal offset of all items, and modify the OffsetY attribute to set the vertical offset of all items.

    **Figure 2**  Modifying the offset of items<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099958_fig1020210122313"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改子项的偏移.png "Modifying the offset of items")

-   Select the List component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399620589.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. In the expanded Feature bar, modify the Direction attribute of the List component to set the arrangement direction of the list.

    **Figure 3**  Modifying the arrangement direction of the list<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099958_fig56791215114412"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改列表的排列方向.png "Modifying the arrangement direction of the list")

-   Select the List component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399740481.png) style icon (Events) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and configure the callback events in the expanded Events bar.

    The events supported by the List component include: OnClick, OnLongPress, OnDrag, OnDragStart, OnDragEnd, OnPress, OnCancel, OnRelease, OnFocus, and OnBlur.

    **Figure 4**  Callback event configuration<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099958_fig186332171075"></a>  
    ![](figures/回调事件配置-1.png "Callback event configuration - 1")

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>-   The MarginBottom and MarginRight attributes under the General page have no rendering effect.
>-   The Isloop, Autoalign, Aligntime, and Startindex attributes under the Feature page have no interface rendering effect. After adding child elements, you can view the effect in the simulator.
>-   Currently, there is an issue where the last element of an item cannot be deleted. To delete it, you can delete the listItemText and listItemPath attributes in index.visual with the interface closed.

#### Label Component<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412317061"></a>

For the usage of common attributes, see the "[Common Attributes of a Component](组件的共有属性.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717660)" section.

-   Select the Label component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399620633.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and modify the Text attribute of the Label component in the expanded Feature bar to edit the text content (the default text content of a newly added component is "default").

    **Figure 1**  Specific attribute configuration: Text content<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619873_fig455274919252"></a>  
    ![](figures/特有属性配置-Text文本内容.png "Specific attribute configuration - Text content")

-   Select the Label component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399740537.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and set the Font attribute of the Label component in the expanded Feature bar:

    -   Color: Font color
    -   Size: Font size
    -   LetterSpace: Letter spacing
    -   LineSpace: Line spacing
    -   LineHeight: Line height (when the line height is smaller than the font size, the font size prevails, and the final line height = line height + line spacing)
    -   TextDirection: Text direction

    **Figure 2**  Common attribute configuration: text-related attributes<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619873_fig15826115915219"></a>  
    ![](figures/通用属性配置-文本相关属性.png "Common attribute configuration - text-related attributes")

-   Select the Label component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399620677.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and modify the TextAlign attribute of the Label component in the expanded Feature bar to change the horizontal and vertical arrangement of the text.

    **Figure 3**  Changing the text arrangement<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619873_fig195425918268"></a>  
    ![](figures/改变文字排布.png "Changing the text arrangement")

-   Select the Label component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399740593.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and modify the LineBreakMode attribute of the Label component in the expanded Feature bar. LineBreakMode corresponds to 6 line-break modes, described in detail as follows:
    -   Adapt: The component size adapts to the text, and the text is not automatically wrapped.

        >![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
        >The component width and height are not affected by the common Width/Height attribute values, so the component width and height cannot be changed by dragging the component or directly modifying the Width/Height values.

        **Figure 4**  LineBreakMode - Adapt<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619873_fig1679161320461"></a>  
        ![](figures/LineBreakMode---Adapt.png "LineBreakMode---Adapt")

    -   Stretch: The component height is displayed according to the set Height value, the width is determined by the longest line in the text, and the text is not automatically wrapped.

        >![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
        >The component width is not affected by the common Width attribute value, so the component width cannot be changed by dragging the component or directly modifying the Width value.

        **Figure 5**  LineBreakMode - Stretch<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619873_fig8791181319462"></a>  
        ![](figures/LineBreakMode---Stretch.png "LineBreakMode---Stretch")

    -   Wrap: The component width is displayed according to the set Width value, the text is automatically wrapped, and the component height is determined by the number of text lines.

        >![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
        >The component height is not affected by the common Height attribute value, so the component height cannot be changed by dragging the component or directly modifying the Height value.

        **Figure 6**  LineBreakMode - Wrap<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619873_fig177911513124619"></a>  
        ![](figures/LineBreakMode---Wrap.png "LineBreakMode---Wrap")

    -   Ellipsis: The component size is displayed according to the set Height and Width values, the text is automatically wrapped, and the text exceeding the component is displayed with an ellipsis at the end.

        **Figure 7**  LineBreakMode - Ellipsis<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619873_fig1179181314462"></a>  
        ![](figures/LineBreakMode---Ellipsis.png "LineBreakMode---Ellipsis")

    -   Clip: The component size is displayed according to the set Height and Width values, the text is automatically wrapped, and the text exceeding the component is automatically hidden at the end.

        **Figure 8**  LineBreakMode - Clip<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619873_fig14791191324610"></a>  
        ![](figures/LineBreakMode---Clip.png "LineBreakMode---Clip")

    -   Marquee: The component size is displayed according to the set Height and Width values, the text is not automatically wrapped, and the text exceeding the component is automatically hidden.

        >![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
        >In this mode, if the length of a single line of text does not exceed the component, the text is aligned according to the set TextAlign.
        >When the text length exceeds the component, the text automatically scrolls to the left in a loop.

        **Figure 9**  LineBreakMode - Marquee<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619873_fig530611174710"></a>  
        ![](figures/LineBreakMode---Marquee.png "LineBreakMode---Marquee")

-   Select the Label component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002365941030.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and modify the RollAnimation attribute of the Label component in the expanded Feature bar.

    The RollAnimation attribute takes effect only when LineBreakMode is Marquee and the text length exceeds the component. RollAnimation includes the scrolling speed (Speed) and the scrolling start position (Pos).

    **Figure 10**  Specific attribute configuration: RollAnimation<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619873_fig19196141194615"></a>  
    ![](figures/特有属性配置-RollAnimation.png "Specific attribute configuration - RollAnimation")

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>-   The MarginBottom and MarginRight attributes under the General page have no interface rendering effect. You can view the effect in the simulator.
>-   The Label component does not support configuring callback events.

#### ToggleButton Component<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378877504"></a>

For the usage of common attributes, see the "[Common Attributes of a Component](组件的共有属性.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717660)" section.

-   Select the ToggleButton component in the canvas and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002366100938.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the ImageOpacity attribute of the ToggleButton component in the expanded Feature bar to change the image transparency.

    **Figure 1**  Modifying the image transparency<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002365940062_fig18996545134711"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改图片透明度.png "Modifying the image transparency")

-   Select the ToggleButton component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002365941090.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the State attribute of the ToggleButton component in the expanded Feature bar to change the component state. When the attribute value is true, the component is in the selected state; otherwise, it is in the unselected state.

    **Figure 2**  Modifying the component state<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002365940062_fig28061923305"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改组件状态.png "Modifying the component state")

-   Select the ToggleButton component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002366100998.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. In the expanded Feature bar, modify the UnSelectedImg attribute of the ToggleButton component to add the image for the unselected state and the SelectedImg attribute to add the image for the selected state. The rendering effect is produced only when both images exist at the same time. You can delete the image by restoring the UnSelectedImg and SelectedImg attributes to empty (deleting them manually).

    >![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
    >The image path does not support Chinese paths and spaces cannot be entered in the path.

    **Figure 3**  Adding images<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002365940062_fig1150615503413"></a>  
    ![](figures/添加图片-2.png "Adding images - 2")

-   Select the ToggleButton component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002365941142.png) style icon (Events) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and configure the callback events in the expanded Events bar.

    The events supported by the ToggleButton component include OnClick, OnLongPress, OnPress, OnCancel, OnRelease, and OnChange.

    **Figure 4**  Callback event configuration<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002365940062_fig560310204504"></a>  
    ![](figures/回调事件配置-3.png "Callback event configuration - 3")

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>The MarginBottom and MarginRight attributes under the General page have no rendering effect for the time being.

For the usage of common attributes, see the "[Common Attributes of a Component](组件的共有属性.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717660)" section.
