#### ScrollView Component<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378877508"></a>

For the usage of common attributes, see the "[Common Attributes of a Component](组件的共有属性.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717660)" section.

-   Select other components in the component bar and drag them into the ScrollView component, then adjust the styles of other components. The position of a child component is relative to the upper-left corner of the parent container. When a component already exists on the interface, clicking the center of the component can drag the component into the container.

    **Figure 1**  Dragging other components into the ScrollView component<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002365940066_fig192648563920"></a>  
    ![](figures/将其他组件拖入ScrollView组件中.png "Dragging other components into the ScrollView component")

-   Select the ScrollView component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621001.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the Direction attribute of the ScrollView component in the expanded Feature bar to control the direction in which the component can scroll.

    **Figure 2**  Modifying the component state<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002365940066_fig1335145711333"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改组件状态-14.png "Modifying the component state - 14")

-   Select the ScrollView component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399740877.png) style icon (Events) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and configure the callback events in the expanded Events bar.

    The events supported by the ScrollView component include OnDrag, OnDragStart, OnDragEnd, OnFocus, OnBlur, and OnScroll.

    **Figure 3**  Callback event configuration<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002365940066_fig159041157202714"></a>  
    ![](figures/回调事件配置-15.png "Callback event configuration - 15")

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>-   When other components are not directly dragged from the UI Control bar on the left, other components may appear outside the ScrollView component and cannot be seen. In addition, when components are dragged from the UI Control bar on the left multiple times, they repeatedly appear at the component position (100, 100).
>-   The MarginBottom and MarginRight attributes under the General page have no rendering effect.
>-   Except the Direction attribute, other attributes under the Feature page have no interface rendering effect. You can view the effect in the simulator.
>-   The scroll bar of the ScrollView is always hidden, and the normal drag mode is still maintained.

#### CircleProgress Component<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412436917"></a>

For the usage of common attributes, see the "[Common Attributes of a Component](组件的共有属性.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717660)" section.

-   Select the CircleProgress component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621005.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. In the expanded Feature bar, modify the LineWidth attribute in the ForegroundStyle and BackgroundStyle windows of the CircleProgress component to change the width of the progress bar, and modify the LineColor attribute to change the color of the progress bar.

    **Figure 1**  Modifying the width of the progress bar<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739749_fig815243422317"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改进度条宽度.png "Modifying the width of the progress bar")

    **Figure 2**  Modifying the color of the progress bar<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739749_fig969345712413"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改进度条颜色.png "Modifying the color of the progress bar")

-   Select the CircleProgress component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002366101078.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. In the expanded Feature bar, modify RangeMax and RangeMin of the CircleProgress component to limit the value of the progress bar, and modify value to change the progress value.

    **Figure 3**  Modifying the progress value<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739749_fig762919369274"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改进度值.png "Modifying the progress value")

-   Select the CircleProgress component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002365941210.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. In the expanded Feature bar, modify the Radius, CenterX, and CenterY attributes of the CircleProgress component to limit the ring.

    **Figure 4**  Modifying the ring size<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739749_fig1581711211515"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改圆环大小.png "Modifying the ring size")

-   Select the CircleProgress component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002366101082.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. In the expanded Feature bar, modify the StartAngel and EndAngel attributes of the CircleProgress component to limit the drawing angle.

    **Figure 5**  Modifying the angle<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739749_fig1267654717373"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改角度.png "Modifying the angle")

-   Select the CircleProgress component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002365941214.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. In the expanded Feature bar, modify the sub-attributes of ForeImage or BackImage of the CircleProgress component to add foreground or background images and modify the image position.

    >![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
    >The image path does not support Chinese paths and spaces cannot be entered in the path.

    **Figure 6**  Adding an image<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739749_fig836417515157"></a>  
    ![](figures/添加图片-16.png "Adding images - 16")

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>-   The MarginBottom and MarginRight attributes under the General page have no rendering effect.
>-   The sub-attributes BackgroundColor, BorderWidth, BorderColor, BorderRadius, LineHeight, and ImageOpacity of ForegroundStyle and BackgroundStyle under the Feature page have no rendering effect.
>-   The CircleProgress component does not support configuring callback events.

#### ArcLabel Component<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717672"></a>

The common attributes of this component do not include the Position and Size attributes. For the usage of other common attributes, see the "[Common Attributes of a Component](组件的共有属性.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717660)" section.

-   Select the ArcLabel component in the canvas and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002366101086.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the LetterSpace attribute of the ArcLabel component in the expanded Feature bar to change the interval between texts.

    **Figure 1**  Modifying the text color<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099966_fig19959125910167"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改文字颜色.png "Modifying the text color")

-   Select the ArcLabel component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002365941218.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and modify the TextAlign attribute of the ArcLabel component in the expanded Feature bar to change the alignment of the text on the ring.

    **Figure 2**  Modifying the text alignment<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099966_fig6210165422115"></a>  
    ![](figures/文本对齐方式修改.png "Modifying the text alignment")

-   Select the ArcLabel component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002366101094.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and modify the Text attribute of the ArcLabel component in the expanded Feature bar to change the displayed text.

    **Figure 3**  Modifying the displayed text<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099966_fig10896149152219"></a>  
    ![](figures/显示文本的修改.png "Modifying the displayed text")

-   Select the ArcLabel component in the canvas and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002365941222.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the TextColor attribute of the ArcLabel component in the expanded Feature bar to change the color of the text.

    **Figure 4**  Modifying the text color<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099966_fig181046357190"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改文字颜色-17.png "Modifying the text color - 17")

-   Select the ArcLabel component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002366101098.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and modify the TextSize of the ArcLabel component in the expanded Feature bar to change the size of the text.

    **Figure 5**  Modifying the text size<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099966_fig13754615153218"></a>  
    ![](figures/文本中文字大小修改.png "Modifying the text size")

-   Select the ArcLabel component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002365941230.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and modify the CenterX and CenterY attributes of the ArcLabel component in the expanded Feature bar to change the center position of the circle where the text is located.

    **Figure 6**  Modifying the center of the circle where the text is located<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099966_fig16333532113418"></a>  
    ![](figures/文本所在圆圆心修改.png "Modifying the center of the circle where the text is located")

-   Select the ArcLabel component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002366101102.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and modify the TextRadius of the ArcLabel component in the expanded Feature bar to change the radius of the circle where the text is located.

    **Figure 7**  Modifying the radius of the circle where the text is located<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099966_fig13296111602013"></a>  
    ![](figures/文本所在圆半径修改.png "Modifying the radius of the circle where the text is located")

-   Select the ArcLabel component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002365941234.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and modify the StartAngle and EndAngle attributes of the ArcLabel component in the expanded Feature bar to change the range in which the text appears on the circle.

    **Figure 8**  Modifying the range in which the text appears on the circle<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099966_fig1867192510273"></a>  
    ![](figures/文本出现在圆上的范围修改.png "Modifying the range in which the text appears on the circle")

-   Select the ArcLabel component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002366101106.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and modify the TextOrientation of the ArcLabel component in the expanded Feature bar to change the orientation of the text.

    **Figure 9**  Modifying the orientation of the text<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099966_fig035742622712"></a>  
    ![](figures/文本朝向修改.png "Modifying the orientation of the text")

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>-   The MarginBottom and MarginRight attributes under the General page have no rendering effect.
>-   The ArcLabel component does not support configuring callback events.

#### SwipeView Component<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412317069"></a>

The common attributes of this component do not include the Border, Margin, and Padding attributes. For the usage of other common attributes, see the "[Common Attributes of a Component](组件的共有属性.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717660)" section.

-   Select the SwipeView component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002365941238.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the Direction attribute of the SwipeView component in the expanded Feature bar to change the scrolling direction of the component. The position of a child component is relative to the upper-left corner of the parent container. When a component already exists on the interface, clicking the center of the component can drag the component into the container.

    **Figure 1**  Modifying the scroll bar position<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619881_fig85221029131212"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改滚动条位置.png "Modifying the scroll bar position")

    ![](figures/1.png)

    >![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
    >The scroll bar is hidden. When the child components in the space exceed the range, they can be dragged. HORIZONTAL means horizontal scrolling, and VERTICAL means vertical scrolling.

-   Select the SwipeView component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621041.png) style icon (Events) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and configure the callback events in the expanded Events bar.

    The events supported by the SwipeView component include: OnDrag, OnDragStart, OnDragEnd, and OnSwipe.

    **Figure 2**  Callback event configuration<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619881_fig968711173355"></a>  
    ![](figures/回调事件配置-18.png "Callback event configuration - 18")

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>-   The Isloop, AlignMode, and TickTime attributes under the Feature page have no rendering effect for the time being.
>-   In horizontal mode, the top alignment ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399740917.png), bottom alignment ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002366101114.png), and vertical centering alignment ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621045.png) in the upper toolbar are unavailable.
>-   In vertical mode, the left alignment ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002365941246.png), right alignment ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399740925.png), and horizontal centering alignment ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002366101122.png) in the upper toolbar are unavailable.
>-   The scroll bar of the SwipeView is always hidden, and the normal drag mode is still maintained.

#### TextureMapper Component<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378877512"></a>

For the usage of common attributes, see the "[Common Attributes of a Component](组件的共有属性.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717660)" section.

-   Select the TextureMapper component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621069.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. In the expanded Feature bar, modify the ImagePath attribute of the TextureMapper component to add an image. You can delete the image by restoring the ImagePath attribute to empty (deleting it manually).

    **Figure 1**  Adding an image<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002365940070_fig15600147102714"></a>  
    ![](figures/添加图片-19.png "Adding an image - 19")

    >![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
    >The ImagePath attribute does not support Chinese paths.

-   Select the TextureMapper component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002366101150.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. In the expanded Feature bar, modify the ImageOpacity attribute of the TextureMapper component to change the transparency of the image.

    **Figure 2**  Modifying the image transparency<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002365940070_fig1859224515129"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改图片透明度-20.png "Modifying the image transparency - 20")

-   Select the TextureMapper component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399740985.png) style icon (Events) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and configure the callback events in the expanded Events bar.

    The events supported by the TextureMapper component include OnMapper.

    **Figure 3**  Callback event configuration<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002365940070_fig833815411471"></a>  
    ![](figures/回调事件配置-21.png "Callback event configuration - 21")

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>-   The MarginBottom and MarginRight attributes under the General page have no rendering effect.
>-   Except the ImagePath attribute, other attributes under the Feature page have no interface rendering effect. You can view the effect in the simulator. Add an image before displaying the animation effect and set the IsStart attribute on the Feature page to true.
>-   After the image is added, the component size is fixed to the size of the image, and modifying the component width and height does not take effect.

#### Slider Component<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412436921"></a>

For the usage of common attributes, see the "[Common Attributes of a Component](组件的共有属性.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717660)" section.

-   Select the Slider component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621121.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the SliderValue attribute of the Slider component in the expanded Feature bar to change the position of the knob.

    **Figure 1**  Modifying the knob position<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739753_fig520210134916"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改滑块位置.png "Modifying the knob position")

-   Select the Slider component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399741001.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the SliderWidth attribute of the Slider component in the expanded Feature bar to change the width of the track.

    **Figure 2**  Modifying the track width<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739753_fig19400118164911"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改滑槽宽度.png "Modifying the track width")

-   Select the Slider component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621137.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the SliderHeight attribute of the Slider component in the expanded Feature bar to change the height of the track. SliderHeight cannot exceed the height of the border.

    **Figure 3**  Modifying the track height<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739753_fig54931822183517"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改滑槽高度.png "Modifying the track height")

-   Select the Slider component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399741017.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the Direction attribute of the Slider component in the expanded Feature bar to change the direction of the track.

    **Figure 4**  Modifying the sliding direction<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739753_fig1164616545365"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改滑方向.png "Modifying the sliding direction")

-   Select the Slider component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621145.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the KnobWidth attribute of the Slider component in the expanded Feature bar to change the width of the knob.

    **Figure 5**  Modifying the knob size<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739753_fig2041221915013"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改滑块大小.png "Modifying the knob size")

-   Select the Slider component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399741033.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. In the expanded Feature bar, modify the BackGroundImage attribute of the Slider component to change the background image of the track, and modify the BackGroundColor attribute of the Slider component to change the background color of the track.

    >![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
    >The image path does not support Chinese paths and spaces cannot be entered in the path.

    **Figure 6**  Adding an image<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739753_fig20840845145018"></a>  
    ![](figures/添加图片-22.png "Adding an image - 22")

    **Figure 7**  Setting the color<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739753_fig1623652355120"></a>  
    ![](figures/设置颜色.png "Setting the color")

-   Select the Slider component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002365941362.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. In the ForeStyle window of the expanded Feature bar, modify the ForeImg attribute of the Slider component to change the foreground image of the track, and modify ForeGroundColor to change the foreground color of the track.

    >![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
    >The image path does not support Chinese paths and spaces cannot be entered in the path.

    **Figure 8**  Adding an image<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739753_fig71361938195220"></a>  
    ![](figures/添加图片-23.png "Adding an image - 23")

    **Figure 9**  Modifying the background color<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739753_fig53181218398"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改背景颜色-24.png "Modifying the background color - 24")

-   Select the Slider component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621173.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. In the KnobStyle window of the expanded Feature bar, modify the KnobImage attribute of the Slider component to add an image, modify the KnobBackGroundColor attribute to change the color of the knob, and modify the KnobRadius attribute to change the border radius of the knob.

    **Figure 10**  Adding an image<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739753_fig4198162695412"></a>  
    ![](figures/添加图片-25.png "Adding an image - 25")

    >![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
    >The image path does not support Chinese paths and spaces cannot be entered in the path.

    **Figure 11**  Modifying the background color<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739753_fig561313503547"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改背景颜色-26.png "Modifying the background color - 26")

    **Figure 12**  Modifying the radius<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739753_fig620717512485"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改弧度.png "Modifying the radius")

-   Select the Slider component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002365941390.png) style icon (Events) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and configure the callback events in the expanded Events bar.

    The events supported by the Slider component include OnClick, OnLongPress, OnDrag, OnDragStart, OnDragEnd, OnPress, OnCancel, OnRelease, OnFocus, and OnBlur.

    **Figure 13**  Callback event configuration<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739753_fig325561020"></a>  
    ![](figures/回调事件配置-27.png "Callback event configuration - 27")

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>-   The GaussianBlur, MarginBottom, and MarginRight attributes under the General page have no rendering effect.
>-   After the image is added, the component size is fixed to the size of the image, and modifying the component width and height does not take effect.

#### Picker Component<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717676"></a>

For the usage of common attributes, see the "[Common Attributes of a Component](组件的共有属性.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717660)" section.

-   Select the Picker component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002366101302.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the Normal attribute of the Picker component in the expanded Feature bar to change the font color of the unselected state of the component.

    **Figure 1**  Modifying the font color of the unselected state<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099970_fig19778846182613"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改未选中字体颜色.png "Modifying the font color of the unselected state")

-   Select the Picker component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002365941414.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the HighLight attribute of the Picker component in the expanded Feature bar to change the font color of the selected state of the component.

    **Figure 2**  Modifying the font color of the selected state<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099970_fig13923214192715"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改选中字体颜色.png "Modifying the font color of the selected state")

-   Select the Picker component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002366101314.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the ItemHeight attribute of the Picker component in the expanded Feature bar to change the item height of the component.

    **Figure 3**  Modifying the item height<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099970_fig1048834002714"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改条目高度.png "Modifying the item height")

-   Select the Picker component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002365941430.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the LoopState attribute of the Picker component in the expanded Feature bar to make the component content loop.

    **Figure 4**  Setting the loop<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099970_fig5493192817"></a>  
    ![](figures/设置循环.png "Setting the loop")

-   Select the Picker component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002366101326.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the Values attribute of the Picker component in the expanded Feature bar to change the text displayed by the component. The data format is: "x1", "x2", "x3"... (the double quotes are required)

    **Figure 5**  Modifying the text<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099970_fig1959462262820"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改文本.png "Modifying the text")

-   Select the Picker component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002365941446.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Synchronize the Count attribute value in the expanded Feature bar (it must be equal to the number of child elements in the input array).

    **Figure 6**  Adding the element count value<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099970_fig9995037194619"></a>  
    ![](figures/添加元素个数值.png "Adding the element count value")

-   Select the Picker component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002366101334.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the TextDirection attribute value of the Picker component in the expanded Feature bar to change the writing direction of the text. LTR indicates that the text direction is from left to right, and RTL indicates from right to left. As shown in [Figure 7](#zh-cn_topic_0000002366099970_fig0726183665610), the first value is "12345". After setting the TextDirection attribute value to RTL, the text displayed in the canvas is "54321".

    **Figure 7**  Modifying the text direction<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099970_fig0726183665610"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改文本方向.png "Modifying the text direction")

-   Select the Picker component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002365941450.png) style icon (Events) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and configure the callback events in the expanded Events bar.

    The events supported by the Picker component include OnFocus and OnBlur.

    **Figure 8**  Callback event configuration<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099970_fig325561020"></a>  
    ![](figures/回调事件配置-28.png "Callback event configuration - 28")

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>-   The MarginBottom and MarginRight attributes under the General page have no rendering effect.
>-   Keep the Count attribute value consistent with the number of array elements.

#### TimePicker Component<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412317073"></a>

For the usage of common attributes, see the "[Common Attributes of a Component](组件的共有属性.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717660)" section.

-   Select the TimePicker component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002366101338.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the ItemHeight attribute of the TimePicker component in the expanded Feature bar to change the item height of the component.

    **Figure 1**  Modifying the item height<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619885_fig13492156123017"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改条目高度-29.png "Modifying the item height - 29")

-   Select the TimePicker component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002365941454.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the Values attribute of the TimePicker component in the expanded Feature bar to change the text displayed by the component.

    **Figure 2**  Modifying the text<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619885_fig1536141613118"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改文本-30.png "Modifying the text - 30")

-   Select the TimePicker component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002366101342.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the HourLoop attribute of the TimePicker component in the expanded Feature bar to change whether the hour window of the component loops.

    **Figure 3**  Setting the loop<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619885_fig172573812312"></a>  
    ![](figures/设置循环-31.png "Setting the loop - 31")

-   Select the TimePicker component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002365941458.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the MinLoop attribute of the TimePicker component in the expanded Feature bar to change whether the minute window of the component loops.

    **Figure 4**  Setting the loop<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619885_fig1741193616326"></a>  
    ![](figures/设置循环-32.png "Setting the loop - 32")

-   Select the TimePicker component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002366101350.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the SecLoop attribute of the TimePicker component in the expanded Feature bar to change whether the second window of the component loops. (When the SecShows attribute is true)

    **Figure 5**  Setting the loop<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619885_fig1031192215331"></a>  
    ![](figures/设置循环-33.png "Setting the loop - 33")

-   Select the TimePicker component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002365941462.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the NormalColor attribute of the TimePicker component in the expanded Feature bar to change the font color of the unselected state of the component.

    **Figure 6**  Modifying the font color<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619885_fig689444916334"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改字体颜色.png "Modifying the font color")

-   Select the TimePicker component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002366101354.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the HighLightColor attribute of the TimePicker component in the expanded Feature bar to change the font color of the selected state of the component.

    **Figure 7**  Modifying the font color<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619885_fig1820476173413"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改字体颜色-34.png "Modifying the font color - 34")

-   Select the TimePicker component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002365941466.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the SecShow attribute of the TimePicker component in the expanded Feature bar to set whether the second window is displayed.

    **Figure 8**  Setting the display of the second window<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619885_fig732614223349"></a>  
    ![](figures/设置秒窗口显示.png "Setting the display of the second window")

-   Select the TimePicker component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002366101358.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the BackgroundFontsize attribute of the TimePicker component in the expanded Feature bar to change the font size of the unselected state of the component.

    **Figure 9**  Setting the font size<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619885_fig7257450143415"></a>  
    ![](figures/设置字体大小.png "Setting the font size")

-   Select the TimePicker component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002365941470.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the HighlightFontsize attribute of the TimePicker component in the expanded Feature bar to change the font size of the selected state of the component.

    **Figure 10**  Setting the font size<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619885_fig1568281294514"></a>  
    ![](figures/设置字体大小-35.png "Setting the font size - 35")

-   Select the TimePicker component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002366101362.png) style icon (Events) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and configure the callback events in the expanded Events bar.

    The events supported by the TimePicker component include OnFocus and OnBlur.

    **Figure 11**  Callback event configuration<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619885_fig325561020"></a>  
    ![](figures/回调事件配置-36.png "Callback event configuration - 36")

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>The MarginBottom and MarginRight attributes under the General page have no rendering effect.

#### Chart Component<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378877516"></a>

For the usage of common attributes, see the "[Common Attributes of a Component](组件的共有属性.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717660)" section.

-   Select the Chart component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002365941478.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the attributes in the XAxis column in the expanded Feature bar to change the style of the horizontal axis.

    -   MarkNum attribute: Changes the number of points on the axis
    -   RangMin and RangMax: Change the value range of the axis
    -   Color: Changes the color of the axis
    -   Visible: Controls whether the axis is visible

    **Figure 1**  Modifying the horizontal axis attributes<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002365940078_fig8661439164210"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改横坐标属性.png "Modifying the horizontal axis attributes")

-   Select the Chart component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621289.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the attributes in the YAxis column in the expanded Feature bar to change the style of the vertical axis. The details are the same as those of the horizontal axis. For details, see [Figure 2](#zh-cn_topic_0000002365940078_fig16661839194210).

    **Figure 2**  Modifying the vertical axis style<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002365940078_fig16661839194210"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改纵坐标样式.png "Modifying the vertical axis style")

-   Select the Chart component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399741189.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. In the expanded Feature bar, click the plus sign on the right of the DataSerial attribute to add data. Multiple groups of data can be added.

    **Figure 3**  Adding data<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002365940078_fig10661113994214"></a>  
    ![](figures/添加数据.png "Adding data")

-   Select the Chart component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621297.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. In the expanded Feature bar, add data and then change the style of the data. The Data attribute is the data to be filled in, the DataCount attribute is the number of data items, FillColor is the color of the fill gradient, LineColor is the color of the line, and the EnableGradient attribute controls whether the gradient color is filled.

    **Figure 4**  Displaying the effect of adding data<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002365940078_fig96611939204217"></a>  
    ![](figures/添加数据效果展示.png "Displaying the effect of adding data")

-   Select the Chart component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399741197.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. In the ColorGradient window of the expanded Feature bar, the MinOpa and MaxOpa attributes change the range of the gradient color. MaxOpa controls the transparency of the upper gradient color, and MinOpa controls the transparency of the lower gradient color.

    **Figure 5**  Modifying the gradient color transparency<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002365940078_fig46611739184215"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改透明度渐变色.png "Modifying the gradient color transparency")

-   Select the Chart component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621305.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the Type attribute of the Chart component in the expanded Feature bar to change the type of the chart.

    **Figure 6**  Modifying the type of the chart<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002365940078_fig19661139164218"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改图的类型.png "Modifying the type of the chart")

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>-   The MarginBottom and MarginRight attributes under the General page have no rendering effect.
>-   The format of the data when adding data is: \{x1,y1\},\{x2,y2\};
>    The data cannot contain spaces, and the horizontal coordinates should increase sequentially starting from 0. In addition, the number in the DataCount attribute cannot be smaller than the number of data items. When the number in the DataCount attribute is larger than the number of data items, it automatically connects back to \{0,0\}. For a bar chart, the MarkNum of the horizontal axis also cannot be smaller than the number of data items.
>-   The ColorGradient attribute is useful only for line charts.
>-   Currently, the Chart component has an issue where the attribute interface cannot change when the DataSerial attributes are modified for the first time. The interface can be changed by modifying other attributes in the DataSerial attributes again.
>-   The Chart component does not support configuring callback events.

#### EditText Component<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412436925"></a>

For the usage of common attributes, see the "[Common Attributes of a Component](组件的共有属性.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717660)" section.

-   Select the EditText component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399741209.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the Placeholder attribute of the EditText component in the expanded Feature bar to change the background hint text.

    **Figure 1**  Modifying the background hint text<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739757_fig688611508216"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改背景提示文字.png "Modifying the background hint text")

-   Select the EditText component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621313.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the Text attribute of the EditText component in the expanded Feature bar to change the content of the foreground text. When the foreground text exists, the background text cannot be displayed.

    **Figure 2**  Modifying the foreground text<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739757_fig18400183213561"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改前景文字.png "Modifying the foreground text")

-   Select the EditText component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399741217.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the EditTextColor attribute of the EditText component in the expanded Feature bar to change the color of the foreground text.

    **Figure 3**  Modifying the color of the foreground text<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739757_fig581421214573"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改前景文字颜色.png "Modifying the color of the foreground text")

-   Select the EditText component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621333.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the MaxLength attribute of the EditText component in the expanded Feature bar to limit the input length of the foreground text.

    **Figure 4**  Limiting the length of the foreground text<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739757_fig8651102555710"></a>  
    ![](figures/限制前景文字长度.png "Limiting the length of the foreground text")

-   Select the EditText component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399741233.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the InputType attribute of the EditText component in the expanded Feature bar to change the input mode.

    -   text mode: The input text is displayed normally
    -   password mode: The input text is displayed in password mode (replaced by "·")

    **Figure 5**  Changing the input mode<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739757_fig13869958175715"></a>  
    ![](figures/改变输入模式.png "Changing the input mode")

-   Select the EditText component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621357.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the PlaceholderColor attribute of the EditText component in the expanded Feature bar to change the color of the background hint text.

    **Figure 6**  Modifying the color of the background hint text<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739757_fig1530813191564"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改背景提示文字颜色.png "Modifying the color of the background hint text")

-   Select the EditText component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399741261.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the CursorColor attribute of the EditText component in the expanded Feature bar to change the color of the cursor.

    **Figure 7**  Cursor color<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739757_fig14857161318582"></a>  
    ![](figures/光标颜色.png "Cursor color")

-   Select the EditText component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621385.png) style icon (Events) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and configure the callback events in the expanded Events bar.

    The events supported by the EditText component include OnClick, OnLongPress, OnPress, OnCancel, and OnRelease.

    **Figure 8**  Callback event configuration<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739757_fig325561020"></a>  
    ![](figures/回调事件配置-37.png "Callback event configuration - 37")

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>-   The MarginBottom and MarginRight attributes under the General page have no rendering effect.
>-   Text modification can only be performed through the specific attributes on the right. When deleting text through the keyboard on the interface, there is an issue where the component is deleted. To modify the foreground text, use the Text field on the right.

#### DigitalClock Component<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717680"></a>

-   Select the DigitalClock component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399741285.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the Color attribute of the DigitalClock component in the expanded Feature bar to change the font color.

#### ImageAnimator Component<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412317077"></a>

For the usage of common attributes, see the "[Common Attributes of a Component](组件的共有属性.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717660)" section.

-   Select the ImageAnimator component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399741305.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Add the image path in the expanded Feature bar.

    **Figure 1**  Image path<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619889_fig28061923305"></a>  
    ![](figures/图片路径.png "Image path")

-   Select the ImageAnimator component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621409.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the ImageNum attribute of the ImageAnimator component in the expanded Feature bar to ensure that the number of displayed images is equal to the number of input image paths.

    **Figure 2**  Number of images<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619889_fig13710344441"></a>  
    ![](figures/图片的个数.png "Number of images")

-   Select the ImageAnimator component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399741313.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the Times attribute of the ImageAnimator component in the expanded Feature bar to control the number of loops. This attribute takes effect only when Repeat is false.

    **Figure 3**  Number of loops<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619889_fig9371123494418"></a>  
    ![](figures/循环次数.png "Number of loops")

-   Select the ImageAnimator component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621413.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the Fixed attribute of the ImageAnimator component in the expanded Feature bar to adapt to the image size.

    **Figure 4**  Whether to adapt to the image size<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619889_fig67661353475"></a>  
    ![](figures/是否适应图片大小.png "Whether to adapt to the image size")

-   Select the ImageAnimator component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399741317.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the Repeat attribute of the ImageAnimator component in the expanded Feature bar to control whether the image loops.

    **Figure 5**  Setting whether the images loop<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619889_fig711219924715"></a>  
    ![](figures/设置图片是否循环.png "Setting whether the images loop")

-   Select the ImageAnimator component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621417.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the Reverse attribute of the ImageAnimator component in the expanded Feature bar to set the loop direction of the images.

    **Figure 6**  Setting the loop direction of the images<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619889_fig137012812132"></a>  
    ![](figures/设置图片循环方向.png "Setting the loop direction of the images")

-   Select the ImageAnimator component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399741321.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the FillMode attribute of the ImageAnimator component in the expanded Feature bar to set the image displayed at the last frame of the animation. true is the first image of the animation in the current loop mode, and false is the last one. The Reverse attribute value affects the order of the images in the loop animation.

    **Figure 7**  Setting the last frame<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619889_fig142541859122517"></a>  
    ![](figures/设置最后一帧.png "Setting the last frame")

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>-   The MarginBottom and MarginRight attributes under the General page have no rendering effect.
>-   The TimeOfUpdate and TimeOfPause attributes under the Feature page have no rendering effect.
>-   The ImageAnimator component does not support configuring callback events.

#### AnalogClock Component<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412436929"></a>

For the usage of common attributes, see the "[Common Attributes of a Component](组件的共有属性.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717660)" section.

-   Select the AnalogClock component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399741329.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. In the expanded Feature bar, modify the PositionX and PositionY attributes in the Hour window to change the position of the hour hand. In addition, the PositionX and PositionY attributes in the Minute and Second windows can separately modify the positions of the minute hand and the second hand.

    **Figure 1**  Modifying the position of the hands<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739761_fig678011363619"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改指针的位置.png "Modifying the position of the hands")

-   Select the AnalogClock component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621429.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. In the expanded Feature bar, modify the CenterX and CenterY attributes in the Hour window to change the rotation center of the hour hand (this coordinate is in the coordinate system with the point where PositionX and PositionY are located as the origin). This coordinate is relative to the position of the hand rather than the upper-left corner of the component. In addition, the CenterX and CenterY attributes in the Minute and Second windows can separately modify the rotation centers of the minute hand and the second hand.

    **Figure 2**  Modifying the rotation center of the hands<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739761_fig821320214408"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改指针的旋转中心.png "Modifying the rotation center of the hands")

-   Select the AnalogClock component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399741333.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. In the expanded Feature bar, modify the Color attribute in the Hour window to change the color of the hour hand. In addition, the Color attributes in the Minute and Second windows can separately modify the colors of the minute hand and the second hand.

    **Figure 3**  Modifying the color of the hands<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739761_fig16383193311422"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改指针的颜色.png "Modifying the color of the hands")

-   Select the AnalogClock component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621433.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. In the expanded Feature bar, modify the Width attribute in the Hour window to change the width of the hour hand. In addition, the Width attributes in the Minute and Second windows can separately modify the widths of the minute hand and the second hand.

    **Figure 4**  Modifying the width of the hands<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739761_fig133511819144416"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改指针的宽度.png "Modifying the width of the hands")

-   Select the AnalogClock component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399741337.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. In the expanded Feature bar, modify the Length attribute in the Hour window to change the length of the hour hand. In addition, the Length attributes in the Minute and Second windows can separately modify the lengths of the minute hand and the second hand.

    **Figure 5**  Modifying the length of the hands<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739761_fig189181575451"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改指针的长度.png "Modifying the length of the hands")

-   Select the AnalogClock component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621437.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. In the expanded Feature bar, modify the Opacity attribute in the Hour window to change the color transparency of the hour hand. In addition, the Opacity attributes in the Minute and Second windows can separately modify the color transparencies of the minute hand and the second hand.

    **Figure 6**  Modifying the color transparency of the hands<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739761_fig1717814924613"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改指针的颜色透明度.png "Modifying the color transparency of the hands")

-   Select the AnalogClock component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399741341.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. In the expanded Feature bar, modify the LineImage attribute in the Hour window to add an image to the hour hand. In addition, the LineImage attributes in the Minute and Second windows can add images to the minute hand and the second hand respectively.

    **Figure 7**  Adding images to the hands<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739761_fig194842357484"></a>  
    ![](figures/为指针添加图片.png "Adding images to the hands")

    >![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
    >The LineImage attribute does not support Chinese paths.

-   Select the AnalogClock component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621445.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Modify the NewMode attribute of the AnalogClock component in the expanded Feature bar to control the display of the second hand.

    **Figure 8**  Controlling the display of the second hand<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739761_fig7341154611512"></a>  
    ![](figures/控制秒针的显示.png "Controlling the display of the second hand")

-   Select the AnalogClock component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399741345.png) style icon (Events) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and configure the callback events in the expanded Events bar.

    The events supported by the AnalogClock component include OnClick, OnLongPress, OnPress, OnCancel, and OnRelease.

    **Figure 9**  Callback event configuration<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739761_fig052871717486"></a>  
    ![](figures/回调事件配置-40.png "Callback event configuration - 40")

-   Select other components in the component bar and drag them into the AnalogClock component, then adjust the styles of other components. The position of a child component is relative to the upper-left corner of the parent container. When a component already exists on the interface, clicking the center of the component can drag the component into the container.

    **Figure 10**  Dragging other components into the AnalogClock component<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739761_fig192648563920"></a>  
    ![](figures/将其他组件拖入AnalogClock组件中.png "Dragging other components into the AnalogClock component")

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>-   The MarginBottom and MarginRight attributes under the General page have no rendering effect.
>-   The Am attribute under the Feature page has no interface rendering effect.

#### SweepClock Component<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717684"></a>

This component contains all the attributes of the AnalogClock component and is similar to it. Therefore, the same attributes are not introduced here. For details, see the "[AnalogClock Component](AnalogClock组件.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412436929)" description.

-   Select the SweepClock component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002365941658.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. In the expanded Feature bar, modify the CenterImage attribute in the Hour window to add an image to the center point of the hour hand. In addition, the CenterImage attributes in the Minute and Second windows can add images to the center points of the minute hand and the second hand respectively.

    **Figure 1**  Adding an image to the center circle<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099982_fig194842357484"></a>  
    ![](figures/为中心圆圈添加图片.png "Adding an image to the center circle")

    >![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
    >The CenterImage attribute does not support Chinese paths.

-   Select the SweepClock component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002366101542.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. In the expanded Feature bar, modify the CenterImageX and CenterImageY attributes in the Hour window to change the position of the center image of the hour hand. In addition, the CenterImage attributes in the Minute and Second windows can separately modify the positions of the center images of the minute hand and the second hand.

    **Figure 2**  Controlling the display of the second hand<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099982_fig7341154611512"></a>  
    ![](figures/控制秒针的显示-41.png "Controlling the display of the second hand - 41")

-   Select other components in the component bar and drag them into the SweepClock component, then adjust the styles of other components. The position of a child component is relative to the upper-left corner of the parent container. When a component already exists on the interface, clicking the center of the component can drag the component into the container.

    **Figure 3**  Dragging other components into the SweepClock component<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099982_fig192648563920"></a>  
    ![](figures/将其他组件拖入SweepClock组件中.png "Dragging other components into the SweepClock component")

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>-   The MarginBottom and MarginRight attributes under the General page have no rendering effect.
>-   The Am attribute under the Feature page has no interface rendering effect.
>-   The SweepClock component does not support configuring callback events.

#### Coverflow Component<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412317081"></a>

The common attributes of this component do not include Border, Margin, and Padding. For the usage of other common attributes, see the "[Common Attributes of a Component](组件的共有属性.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717660)" section.

-   Select the Coverflow component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399741357.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. Import one or more images in the expanded Feature bar (you can adjust the canvas size to make the image display effect better). The first image is located in the center of the canvas by default.

    **Figure 1**  Specific attribute configuration: ImagePath<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619893_fig144651235202314"></a>  
    ![](figures/特有属性配置-ImagePath-42.png "Specific attribute configuration - ImagePath - 42")

    >![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
    >ImagePath does not support Chinese paths.
    >The imported Bin file must be a Bin converted from an image; otherwise, the simulator will behave abnormally.

-   Select the Coverflow component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621461.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style on the right, and modify ImgWidth and ImgHeight of the Coverflow component in the expanded Feature bar to adjust the image width and height.

    After the image width and height are changed, the width and height of all images imported into the Coverflow component change accordingly, and the first image is always located in the center of the component.

    **Figure 2**  Specific attribute configuration: ImgWidth and ImgHeight<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619893_fig1549113615248"></a>  
    ![](figures/特有属性配置-ImgWidth和ImgHeight.png "Specific attribute configuration - ImgWidth and ImgHeight")

-   Select the Coverflow component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399741361.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style on the right, and modify RotateAngle of the Coverflow component in the expanded Feature bar to adjust the rotation angle.

    **Figure 3**  Specific attribute configuration: RotateAngle<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619893_fig1917017159243"></a>  
    ![](figures/特有属性配置-RotateAngle.png "Specific attribute configuration - RotateAngle")

-   Select the Coverflow component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621465.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style on the right, and modify Padding of the Coverflow component in the expanded Feature bar to adjust the spacing between images.

    **Figure 4**  Specific attribute configuration: Padding<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619893_fig2142192232415"></a>  
    ![](figures/特有属性配置-Padding.png "Specific attribute configuration - Padding")

-   Select the Coverflow component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399741365.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style on the right, and modify IsShowMirrorImg of the Coverflow component in the expanded Feature bar to set whether to display the image mirror.

    **Figure 5**  Specific attribute configuration: IsShowMirrorImg<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619893_fig25262913248"></a>  
    ![](figures/特有属性配置-IsShowMirrorImg.png "Specific attribute configuration - IsShowMirrorImg")

-   Select the Coverflow component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621473.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style on the right, and modify MirrorOpa of the Coverflow component in the expanded Feature bar to set the transparency of the image mirror.

    **Figure 6**  Specific attribute configuration: MirrorOpa<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619893_fig96581036102413"></a>  
    ![](figures/特有属性配置-MirrorOpa.png "Specific attribute configuration - MirrorOpa")

-   Select the Coverflow component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399741373.png) style icon (Events) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and configure the callback events in the expanded Events bar.

    The events supported by the Coverflow component include OnDrag, OnDragStart, OnDragEnd, and OnCoverflow.

    **Figure 7**  Callback event configuration<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619893_fig4163730162310"></a>  
    ![](figures/回调事件配置-43.png "Callback event configuration - 43")

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>-   RotateAngle, IsShowMirrorImg, and MirrorOpa under the Feature page have no rendering effect for now. You can view the specific effect in the simulator.
>-   The callback function of the Coverflow callback event is named "OnScroll()" in the generated XXXPresenter.cpp file.

#### Barcode Component<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378877524"></a>

For the usage of common attributes, see the "[11.1.2.2 Common Attributes of a Component](组件的共有属性.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717660)" section.

Select the Barcode component in the canvas and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002365941686.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. In the expanded Feature bar, there is a BarcodeInfo attribute that provides the Barcode information. It cannot be empty. If it is set to empty, the barcode fails to be displayed in the simulator after the code is generated.

**Figure 1**  Specific attribute configuration: BarcodeInfo<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002365940086_fig191081212257"></a>  
![](figures/特有属性配置-BarcodeInfo.png "Specific attribute configuration - BarcodeInfo")

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>-   The BarcodeInfo attribute under the Feature page has no page rendering effect.
>-   The Barcode component does not support configuring callback events.
>-   BarcodeInfo cannot be set to empty and does not support Chinese characters. If it is set to empty or to Chinese characters, the barcode fails to be displayed in the simulator.
>-   After setting BarcodeInfo, click another position and then generate the code.

#### CardPage Component<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412436933"></a>

For the General attributes of this component, see the description in "[11.1.2.2 Common Attributes of a Component](组件的共有属性.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717660)".

-   Select the CardPage component in the canvas and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621509.png)  style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. In the expanded Feature bar, there is an IsCoverable attribute, which defaults to false. It determines whether the previous CardPage component is covered when another CardPage component is dragged in and displayed.

    **Figure 1**  Specific attribute configuration: IsCoverable<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739765_fig195722386343"></a>  
    ![](figures/特有属性配置-IsCoverable.png "Specific attribute configuration - IsCoverable")

-   Select the CardPage component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002366101610.png) style icon (Events) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and configure the callback events in the expanded Events bar.

    The event types supported by the CardPage component include OnClick, OnLongPress, OnDrag, OnDragStart, OnDragEnd, OnPress, OnCancel, OnRelease, OnFocus, OnBlur, OnRotate, OnRotateStart, and OnRotateEnd.

    **Figure 2**  Callback event configuration<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739765_fig1790072115352"></a>  
    ![](figures/回调事件配置-44.png "Callback event configuration - 44")

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>-   The attributes under the Feature page have no interface rendering effect.
>-   CardPage is a container component and is mainly used as a child component of CrossView.

#### CrossView Component<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717688"></a>

For the General attributes of this component, see the description in "[11.1.2.2 Common Attributes of a Component](组件的共有属性.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717660)".

-   Select the CrossView component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399741433.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. In the expanded Feature bar, you can see the EnableScreenCap attribute. A drop-down box for setting the bool variable value is displayed, which determines whether the screenshot mode is enabled when switching cards.

    **Figure 1**  Specific attribute configuration: EnableScreenCap<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099986_fig1358134319354"></a>  
    
    ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002366101638.png)

-   Select the CrossView component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002365941766.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. In the expanded Feature bar, you can see the SetAnimatorTime attribute. It determines the release animation time in the simulator, in milliseconds (ms).

    **Figure 2**  Specific attribute configuration: SetAnimatorTime<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099986_fig2959881495"></a>  
    
    ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002366101662.png)

-   Select the CrossView component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621581.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. In the expanded Feature bar, you can see the DragDirection attribute. It determines the direction from which child components are dragged in.

    **Figure 3**  Specific attribute configuration: DragDirection<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099986_fig1772818954716"></a>  
    
    ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002365941782.png)

    When the dragged-in component is not of the CardPage type, the message "Only supports cardpage component" or "Only CardPage components can be dragged in" is displayed in the lower-right corner, and the dragged-in child component is not displayed on the canvas.

    **Figure 4**  Specific attribute configuration: DragDirection (only CardPage-type child components can be dragged in)<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099986_fig634965194814"></a>  
    ![](figures/特有属性配置-DragDirection（仅支持拖拽CardPage类型的子组件）.png "Specific attribute configuration - DragDirection (only CardPage-type child components can be dragged in)")

    The front-end rendering interface of the CrossView component displays only one of its CardPage child components:

    -   When a CardPage component is added, the most recently dragged-in CardPage component is displayed by default, and the previous ones are hidden.
    -   When a CardPage component is deleted, the CardPage component dragged in last time is displayed by default, and the others are hidden.

    To implement the switching, click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002366101686.png) button on the Panel panel described in "[GUI Drag Interface Description](GUI拖拽界面介绍.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412317053)". The usage of this button is as follows:

    1.  Left/right switching displays the previous and next dragged-in CardPage child components respectively.
    2.  Whether cyclic switching between components is supported is consistent with the setting rules of the simulator. You can select True or False for Isloop in the Feature bar. When it is set to False, clicking the left button on the first component and the right button on the last component has no switching effect. When it is set to True, clicking the left button on the first component and the right button on the last component switches between them.
    3.  The switching buttons take effect only when a CardPage child component whose parent component is CrossView is selected. To select it, click the CardPage in the component tree on the left or click the CardPage on the rendering interface, and then click the left and right buttons to display the previous and next components of the selected component, as shown in [Figure 5](#zh-cn_topic_0000002366099986_fig10608162418201).
    4.  If the selected component is not a CardPage child component whose parent component is CrossView, a pop-up message is displayed, as shown in [Figure 6](#zh-cn_topic_0000002366099986_fig560918241207).

    **Figure 5**  Specific attribute configuration: DragDirection (CardPage switching display logic)<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099986_fig10608162418201"></a>  
    ![](figures/特有属性配置-DragDirection（CardPage切换展示逻辑）.png "Specific attribute configuration - DragDirection (CardPage switching display logic)")

    **Figure 6**  Specific attribute configuration: DragDirection (pop-up reminder for switching non-CrossView child components)<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099986_fig560918241207"></a>  
    ![](figures/特有属性配置-DragDirection（非CrossView子组件切换的弹窗提醒）.png "Specific attribute configuration - DragDirection (pop-up reminder for switching non-CrossView child components)")

    When DragDirection is set to Horizontal, there is no limit on the number of CardPage components to drag in. Each time a new CardPage component is dragged in, the other CardPage child components are hidden.

    **Figure 7**  Specific attribute configuration: DragDirection (CardPage-type child components dragged in from the horizontal direction)<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099986_fig12609112419209"></a>  
    ![](figures/特有属性配置-DragDirection（水平方向拖拽来的CardPage类型子组件）.png "Specific attribute configuration - DragDirection (CardPage-type child components dragged in from the horizontal direction)")

    When DragDirection is set to Vertical-Up, dragging in a new CardPage component also hides the other CardPage child components. However, only one component can be dragged in. When you drag in another CardPage child component in this direction, the message "UpPage direction only supports one component" or "Only one CardPage component can be dragged in the upward direction" is displayed in the lower-right corner.

    **Figure 8**  Specific attribute configuration: DragDirection (only one child component can be dragged in the upward direction)<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099986_fig1260952419208"></a>  
    ![](figures/特有属性配置-DragDirectio（竖直上方向仅支持拖拽一个子组件）.png "Specific attribute configuration - DragDirectio (only one child component can be dragged in the upward direction)")

    When DragDirection is set to Vertical-Down, dragging in a new CardPage component also hides the other CardPage child components, and only one component can be dragged in. When you drag in another CardPage child component in this direction, the message "DownPage direction only supports one component" or "Only one CardPage component can be dragged in the downward direction" is displayed in the lower-right corner.

    **Figure 9**  Specific attribute configuration: DragDirection (only one child component can be dragged in the downward direction)<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099986_fig16609142412208"></a>  
    ![](figures/特有属性配置-DragDirection（竖直下方向仅支持拖拽一个子组件）.png "Specific attribute configuration - DragDirection (only one child component can be dragged in the downward direction)")

-   Select the CrossView component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002365941822.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. In the expanded Feature bar, click the Isloop attribute. A drop-down box for setting the bool variable value is displayed, which determines the horizontal card loop mode.

    **Figure 10**  Specific attribute configuration: Isloop<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099986_fig18540511191815"></a>  
    
    ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399741541.png)

-   Select the CrossView component. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002366101718.png) style icon (Events) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and configure the callback events in the expanded Events bar.

    The events supported by the CrossView component include OnClick, OnLongPress, OnDrag, OnDragStart, OnDragEnd, OnPress, OnCancel, OnRelease, OnFocus, OnBlur, OnRotate, OnRotateStart, OnRotateEnd, and OnSwipe. OnSwipe defaults to True.

    **Figure 11**  Callback event configuration<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366099986_fig5540511151817"></a>  
    
    ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621633.png)

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>-   On the Feature page, except for the DragDirection feature and Isloop, which are associated with the front-end rendering of the GUI interface, the other attributes have no interface rendering effect. You can view them in the simulator.
>-   The CrossView component supports dragging in only CardPage-type child components.
>-   All pop-up messages of the CrossView component support both Chinese and English.

#### MapView Component<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412317085"></a>

For the General attributes of this component, see the description in "[11.1.2.2 Common Attributes of a Component](组件的共有属性.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717660)".

-   Select the MapView component and click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002365941846.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style bar on the right. In the expanded Feature bar, click the icon on the right of SvgPath. An interface for selecting an Svg file is displayed. Decompress the Svg file corresponding to the map to any directory, click to select it, and then open the directory again and refresh it. You can see that a bin file with the same name as the Svg file is generated. This file is read and displayed by the simulator. Meanwhile, the map file is drawn on the canvas.

    **Figure 1**  Specific attribute configuration: SvgPath - Selecting an Svg file<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619897_fig1556381810504"></a>  
    ![](figures/特有属性配置-SvgPath-选择Svg文件.png "Specific attribute configuration - SvgPath - Selecting an Svg file")

    **Figure 2**  Specific attribute configuration: SvgPath - Drawing an Svg image<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619897_fig761210910508"></a>  
    ![](figures/特有属性配置-SvgPath-绘制Svg图片.png "Specific attribute configuration - SvgPath - Drawing an Svg image")

-   Select the MapView component. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621645.png) style icon (Events) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and configure the callback events in the expanded Events bar.

    The events supported by the MapView component include OnClick, OnLongPress, OnDrag, OnDragStart, OnDragEnd, OnPress, OnCancel, OnRelease, OnFocus, OnBlur, OnRotate, OnRotateStart, OnRotateEnd, and OnPOIClick.

    **Figure 3**  Callback event configuration<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619897_fig982152854217"></a>  
    ![](figures/回调事件配置-45.png "Callback event configuration - 45")

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>-   MapView is a map component. You should display the map file on the canvas and in the simulator.
>-   The General attributes of MapView contain only two options, ID and Position, for users to set. The tool and the simulator display the original size of the Svg image by default. You can adjust the Position parameter based on the actual effect in the simulator.

    ![](figures/回调事件配置-48.png "Callback event configuration - 48")

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:**

    After the image width and height are changed, the width and height of all images imported into the HexagonsList component change accordingly, and the first image is always located in the center of the component.

    **Figure 2**  Specific attribute configuration: ImageWidth and ImageHeight<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739769_fig1549113615248"></a>

    **Figure 3**  Specific attribute configuration: Padding<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739769_fig2142192232415"></a>  
    ![](figures/特有属性配置-Padding-51.png "Specific attribute configuration - Padding - 51")

-   Select the HexagonsList component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621673.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style on the right, and modify the OriImgDistance attribute of the HexagonsList component in the expanded Feature bar to set the original image distance between the centers of two adjacent images in the simulator.

-   Select the HexagonsList component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399741589.png) style icon (Feature) in the attribute and style on the right, and modify the ScrollBlankSize attribute of the HexagonsList component in the expanded Feature bar to set the blank size when the component scrolls in the simulator.

    **Figure 7**  Specific attribute configuration: ScrollBlankSize<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739769_fig1256315491283"></a>  
    ![](figures/特有属性配置-ScrollBlankSizec.png "Specific attribute configuration - ScrollBlankSizec")

-   Select the HexagonsList component in the canvas. Use the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621689.png) style icon (Events) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and configure the callback events in the expanded Events bar.

    The events supported by the HexagonsList component include OnClick, OnLongPress, OnDrag, OnDragStart, OnDragEnd, OnPress, OnCancel, OnRelease, OnFocus, OnBlur, OnRotate, OnRotateStart, and OnRotateEnd.

    **Figure 8**  Callback event configuration<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739769_fig17698142911119"></a>  
    ![](figures/回调事件配置-52.png "Callback event configuration - 52")

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>OriImgDistance, ImgSizeInCenter, ReboundedSize, and ScrollBlankSize under the Feature page have no rendering effect for now. You can view the specific effect in the simulator.
>ImageWidth, Padding, and ImageHeight can be used to set the image size when multiple components are displayed on the interface.
>The initial values of Rebounded and ScrollBlankSize are 227, when the default component Position is \(0, 0, 454, 454\), that is, the entire dial. The specific value can be adjusted based on the actual display effect in the simulator. Usually, this value is half of the component width.
>The product of the OriImgDistance and ImgSizeInCenter attributes and the component width determines the layout position relationship of the images in the simulator.

    **Figure 3**  Specific attribute configuration: IsShowMirrorImg<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619905_fig25262913248"></a>

    When the dragged-in component is not of the TransformGroup type, the message "Only supports transformgroup component" or "Only TransformGroup components can be dragged in" is displayed in the lower-right corner, and the dragged-in child component is not displayed on the canvas.

    **Figure 5**  Dragging a component into the Coverflow2 component<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619905_fig1535915143218"></a>  
    ![](figures/组件拖入Coverflow2组件中.png "Dragging a component into the Coverflow2 component")

The front-end rendering interface of the Coverflow2 component displays only one of its TransformGroup child components:

-   When a TransformGroup component is added, the most recently dragged-in TransformGroup component is displayed by default, and the previous ones are hidden.
-   When a TransformGroup component is deleted, the TransformGroup component dragged in last time is displayed by default, and the others are hidden.

To implement the switching, click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002365941974.png) button on the Panel panel described in "[GUI Drag Interface Description](GUI拖拽界面介绍.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412317053)". The usage of this button is as follows:

1.  Left/right switching displays the previous and next dragged-in TransformGroup child components respectively.
2.  The switching buttons take effect when a TransformGroup child component whose parent component is Coverflow2 is selected. To select it, click the TransformGroup in the component tree on the left or click the TransformGroup on the rendering interface, and then click the left and right buttons to display the previous and next components of the selected component, as shown in [Figure 6](#zh-cn_topic_0000002399619905_fig14330923143518).
3.  If the selected component is not a TransformGroup child component whose parent component is Coverflow2, a pop-up message is displayed, as shown in [Figure 7](#zh-cn_topic_0000002399619905_fig1459413716407).

**Figure 6**  TransformGroup switching display logic<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619905_fig14330923143518"></a>  
![](figures/TransformGroup切换展示逻辑.png "TransformGroup switching display logic")

**Figure 7**  Pop-up reminder for switching non-Coverflow2 child components<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619905_fig1459413716407"></a>  
![](figures/非Coverflow2子组件切换的弹窗提醒.png "Pop-up reminder for switching non-Coverflow2 child components")
