#### User Code Editing<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412436945"></a>

Click "Convert to C++ files" in the upper-right corner of the canvas. The code corresponding to the interface style is generated in the project files.

**Figure 1**  Generate code button<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739777_fig36701735185010"></a>  
![](figures/生成代码按钮.png "Generate code button")

Take the aaaa.gui project named aaaa and the Button component as an example. The generated code directory is located at "application/wearable/nativeapp/gui project name" under the project directory, and the specific structure is shown in [Figure 2](#zh-cn_topic_0000002399739777_fig084021814113). You can add your own callback events in the xxxPresenter.cpp file according to the template. The template code is shown in [Figure 3](#zh-cn_topic_0000002399739777_fig113311395104). The rest is generated automatically and is refreshed every time the generate code button is clicked.

**Figure 2**  Directory structure of the generated code<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739777_fig084021814113"></a>  
![](figures/生成代码目录结构.png "Directory structure of the generated code")

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>-   When you switch from another component to the CrossView component, or switch from the CrossView component to another component, and then generate the code, due to the particularity of adding child components to the CrossView component, you need to manually delete the existing "/src/$\{AppName\}View.cpp" first, and then click to convert and generate the code.
>-   The other files in the "ui\_xxView.h" format cannot be modified. The GUI overwrites and regenerates them every time the generate code button is clicked.

**Figure 3**  Template code of the callback event<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739777_fig113311395104"></a>  
![](figures/回调事件模板代码.png "Template code of the callback event")

Take the click callback event as an example. If only one component in the current canvas has the click callback set to true, you can directly add the callback event in the OnClick callback function. If multiple components in the current canvas have the click callback set to true, you need to distinguish the components according to the prompt to implement their respective callbacks, as shown in [Figure 4](#zh-cn_topic_0000002399739777_fig171118207109).

**Figure 4**  Code implementation of the same callback event for multiple components<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739777_fig171118207109"></a>  
![](figures/多个组件同一回调事件代码实现.png "Code implementation of the same callback event for multiple components")

#### Component Alignment<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717700"></a>

Component alignment is implemented by selecting a component and then clicking the alignment button ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399741701.png) in the toolbar at the top of the interface.

The alignment effects include left alignment ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002366101870.png), left-right center alignment ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621797.png), right alignment ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002365942006.png), top alignment ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399741709.png), top-bottom center alignment ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002366101906.png), and bottom alignment ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621837.png).

Based on the number of selected components, the alignment operation is divided into two types:

-   Select one component

    When only one component is selected, the selected component is used as the component to be aligned, and its parent component is used as the reference for alignment. You can view the parent-child relationship of components in the component tree.

    >![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
    >It is invalid when only the Root component is selected.

-   Select multiple components (click multiple components while holding down the Ctrl key)

    When more than one component is selected, the last selected component is used as the reference, and the other selected components are aligned as the components to be aligned.

    >![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
    >The selected components can only be the Root component or its direct child components.

#### Component Hierarchy Movement<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412317097"></a>

Component hierarchy movement is implemented by selecting a component and then clicking the hierarchy movement button in the toolbar at the top of the interface.

**Figure 1**  Component hierarchy movement<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619909_fig84841225105411"></a>  
![](figures/组件层级移动.png "Component hierarchy movement")

![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002366101930.png) moves the component forward one layer, and ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621861.png) moves the component backward one layer.

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>-   Each click can move the component forward/backward only one layer.
>-   When multiple components are selected at the same time, the hierarchy movement operation takes effect only on the last selected component.
>-   Hierarchy movement cannot adjust the parent component of the current component (for example, if the current component is a child component of ScrollView/SwipeView, the hierarchy movement operation cannot move the component out).
>-   In the component tree, the closer a component is to the top, the lower its hierarchy; the closer it is to the bottom, the higher its hierarchy.

#### Gaussian Blur Attribute<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378877540"></a>

To set the Gaussian blur attribute of a component, click the ![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002365942066.png) style icon (General) in the attribute and style bar on the right, and modify the GaussianBlur attribute of the component in the expanded General bar, as shown in [Figure 1](#zh-cn_topic_0000002365940102_fig12812852164217).

**Figure 1**  Gaussian blur<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002365940102_fig12812852164217"></a>  
![](figures/高斯模糊-63.png "Gaussian blur - 63")

After the Gaussian blur is set for a component, the other components whose hierarchy is lower than that of this component are blurred, as shown in [Figure 2](#zh-cn_topic_0000002365940102_fig17715201710478).

**Figure 2**  Example of the Gaussian blur effect<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002365940102_fig17715201710478"></a>  
![](figures/高斯模糊效果范例.png "Example of the Gaussian blur effect")

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>-   The Gaussian blur takes effect only when FullScreenState is true and GaussianBlur is not 0 at the same time.
>-   Due to different rendering methods, the component rendering effect in the GUI interface differs to some extent from the actual effect in the simulator.
>-   When multiple components are all set with the Gaussian blur effect, only the Gaussian blur of the lowest component takes effect.
>-   The component hierarchy can be viewed in the component tree. For the hierarchy movement method and detailed description, see "[Component Hierarchy Movement](组件层级移动.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412317097)".
>-   The GaussianBlur attribute determines the degree of the Gaussian blur effect. A reasonable range is \[0, 32\].
>-   Event addition: In the current version, enabling an event in the Events attribute of a control does not generate event code. You need to manually add the event code. The specific method is to add the expected event to the specified control in the setupUi method of the application code (take an application named gui6 as an example, at sdk root directory/application/wearable/nativeapp/nativeui/gui6/include/ui\_gui6View.h). For example, to add the OnLongPress event to the control whose id name is "button1", add "button1-\>SetOnLongPressListener\(gui6presenter\_\);" at the end of the setupUi method, where "button1\_" is the variable name of the control in the code, and "gui6presenter\_" is the automatically generated pointer in the code associated with the application name.

## Methods for Displaying the Simulator Effect<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412436949"></a>





### Running the Demo<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717704"></a>

Click "App Debug" in the figure. The compilation and debugging are started and the simulator is displayed. The effect of running the default demo is shown in [Figure 2](#zh-cn_topic_0000002366100002_fig771416101317).

The log information during the running process is recorded in the "simulator\_build/error.txt" file under the root directory of the currently opened project.

**Figure 1**  App Debug button<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366100002_fig599934320100"></a>  
![](figures/App-Debug按钮.png "App Debug button")

**Figure 2**  Display of the simulator effect<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366100002_fig771416101317"></a>  
![](figures/模拟器效果展示.png "Display of the simulator effect")

### Debugging and Running<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412317101"></a>

After clicking the "App Debug" button, the compilation and debugging functions are automatically started. You can add breakpoints and debug as needed. For the debugging function, see the "[8 Project Debugging](工程调试.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378877472)" chapter.

**Figure 1**  Display of the debugging function<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619913_fig28521758191013"></a>  
![](figures/调试功能展示.png "Display of the debugging function")

### Running the Code Generated Through GUI Drag-and-Drop<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378877544"></a>

After adding components, changing attributes, or enabling callbacks on the canvas and starting the simulator, you can follow the operation in [Figure 1](#zh-cn_topic_0000002365940106_fig18197143416124) to view the function list added on the canvas.

**Figure 1**  Viewing the added component functions from the simulator<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002365940106_fig18197143416124"></a>  
![](figures/从模拟器查看添加的组件功能.png "Viewing the added component functions from the simulator")

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>When the simulator displays the main interface, clicking the button in the upper-right corner automatically jumps to the function list page, and the bottom of the list is the added component list. When the simulator displays a non-main interface, clicking the button in the upper-right corner automatically jumps to the main interface.

As can be seen from the figure, the name of the currently added function defaults to the name of the created GUI project, and the icon defaults to the "Example" icon. To modify the icon and name of the GUI project in the simulator, you can modify them in the "Register.cpp" file in the generated code directory of the corresponding GUI project.

**Figure 2**  Modifying the default GUI project name and the icon before the list<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002365940106_fig21411933131311"></a>  
![](figures/修改默认GUI工程名称和列表前的图标.png "Modifying the default GUI project name and the icon before the list")

In the corresponding cpp file, you can modify the icon or name of the corresponding component in the simulator by modifying these four parameters.

①: Icon displayed when the icon mode (default mode) is selected on the desktop. The file format is .bin.

②: Icon displayed when the honeycomb mode is selected on the desktop. The file format is .bin.

③: Icon displayed when the football mode is selected on the desktop. The file format is .bin.

④: Name corresponding to the component.

For the icon mode selected by default, you only need to change the parameters in ① and ④ to modify the icon and component name corresponding to the component in the simulator.

### Setting the Simulator Size and Shape<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412436953"></a>

After creating a GUI project, if you want to change the size and shape of the canvas or simulator again, you can search for the "graphic\_config.h" file in the project. It is located in the "application/brandy/simulator/libui" directory under the project directory. Change the three parameters HORIZONTAL\_RESOLUTION, VERTICAL\_RESOLUTION, and DEVICE\_SHAPE, which correspond to the diameter (length and width) and the shape of the simulator respectively.

**Figure 1**  Changing the size and shape of the simulator<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739785_fig5357185214137"></a>  
![](figures/更改模拟器的尺寸与形状.png "Changing the size and shape of the simulator")

## Introduction to Graphics Tools<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717708"></a>

Click the "Graphics Tools" icon on the plugin page or in the status bar.

**Figure 1**  "Graphics Tools" icon on the plugin page or in the status bar<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366100006_fig135900300384"></a>  

![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002404301777.png)

A function tab is displayed, which currently contains two functions: "Image Decompression" and "Video First-Frame Extraction".

**Figure 2**  "GUI Graphics Tools" tab<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366100006_fig5647114212381"></a>  

![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002488087200.png)






### Environment Configuration<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412317105"></a>

Before using the "Graphics Tools" function, you need to configure the Python environment. For details, see "[Toolchain Configuration](工具链toolchain配置.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412436857)".

After Python is installed successfully, you need to install the dependency modules of pip.

-   opencv module
-   numpy module
-   Pillow module
-   matplotlib module

After the installation is complete, open cmd in the "tools/python/Scripts" directory under the VS Code installation directory and run "pip.exe list" to check whether each dependency is installed successfully.

**Figure 1**  Checking the installation of pip dependencies<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619917_fig138293814396"></a>  

![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002415229650.png)

### Image Decompression Tool<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378877548"></a>

Functions of the image decompression tool: based on the compression algorithm and pixel format information configured in the xml file, pack each subdirectory in the specified directory into a resource file and generate the resource index file "ImageResources.cpp"; or pack each image file in the specified directory into a resource file. In the decompression mode, the images in .bin files are decompressed into images in .png format. In the compression mode, the images in .png, .jpg, or .bmp format are compressed into the .bin file format.

**Figure 1**  Image decompression interface<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002365940110_fig2012249183513"></a>  
![](figures/图片解压缩界面.png "Image decompression interface")

-   Script path: Select the folder path where the "images\_convert.py" script is located, generally "tools\\DebugKits\\graphic\_tools\\image\_converter\_tool".
-   Decompression/compression mode: Specify the decompression or compression mode. The compression mode is selected by default.
-   Packing method: It is divided into the file packing method and the folder packing method. The file packing method is selected by default.
    -   File packing method: Pack each image as a unit.
    -   Folder packing method: After packing the images, pack the packed images contained in each subfolder together as a unit.

-   Compression mode: Manual compression mode by default.
-   alpha compression mode: 0: uncompressed; 1: compressed. Default value: 1 (compressed).
-   rgb compression mode: 0: uncompressed; 1: compressed. Default value: 1 (compressed).
-   Compression unit width: 4--4x4; 6--6x4; 8--8x4; 16--16x4. It is also called the compression ratio. In the ARGB8888 format, it corresponds to 4x, 6x, 8x, and 16x compression respectively. In RGB888, it corresponds to 3x, 4.5x, 6x, and 12x compression respectively. In RGB565, it corresponds to 2x, 3x, 4x, and 8x compression respectively. Default value: 6x compression.
-   Image input path: Specify the root directory of the images to be processed. The images in the image directory can be in formats such as ".jpg, .png, and .bmp".
-   Image output path: Specify the output folder.

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-notice.gif) **Notice:** 
>-   Image compression supports only the compression of .png, .bmp, and .jpg files (the file suffix cannot be uppercase), and the image resolution cannot exceed 1280\*800 (width\*height).
>-   The subfolder names and file names must be a combination of digits and letters.
>-   When the decompression/compression mode is set to the decompression mode, the compression mode, alpha compression mode, rgb compression mode, and compression unit width options are hidden.
>-   When both alpha and rgb are 0, it is the uncompressed mode. Pay attention to the adaptation.
>-   The compression ratio is compared with the size of the raw data rather than the size of the source file. For example, for a 466×466 RGBA image, the uncompressed size is 848 KB, the size is 142 KB with 6x compression, and approximately 54 KB with 16x compression.
>-   The compression operation is lossy compression. The larger the compression ratio, the greater the image loss.

**Compression Mode Example<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002365940110_section19664105194515"></a>**

### Video First-Frame Extraction Tool<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412436957"></a>

Before the video is loaded, the first frame is a black frame. You need to manually set a starting preview image. This function provides the capability to extract the first frame of the video and convert it into an image in BMP format.

**Figure 1**  Video first-frame extraction tool page<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739789_fig111561422134515"></a>  
![](figures/视频首帧提取工具页面.png "Video first-frame extraction tool page")

-   Script path: Select the folder path where the "convert\_2\_bmp.py" script is located, generally "tools\\DebugKits\\graphic\_tools\\convert\_2\_bmp".
-   Video path: The path of the video file to be extracted.
-   Video frame width/video frame height: Specify the width and height of the video frame. They must be consistent with the video resource and must be manually entered.
-   Image generation path: The path for generating the image.

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-caution.gif) **Caution:** 
>-   Currently, only YUV444 and YUV420 video formats are supported.
>-   The video supports only MJPEG encoding and .mp4 packaging. You can use the ffmpeg tool to adjust the video format and size. Example: "ffmpeg -i video.mp4 -vf crop=454:454 -b:v 4228k -vcodec mjpeg out.mp4".
>-   The video frame width/height can be viewed in the properties--\>details of the video. If the video frame width and height are inconsistent with the actual values, the generated image will be distorted.

Video first-frame extraction example:

**Figure 2**  Specific option example of video first-frame extraction<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739789_fig28171353184518"></a>  
![](figures/视频首帧提取具体选项样例.png "Specific option example of video first-frame extraction")

After all options are selected, click Done to execute the video first-frame extraction function. The output displayed on the terminal interface is as follows:

**Figure 3**  Terminal interface output during video first-frame extraction<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739789_fig1474664164615"></a>  
![](figures/视频首帧提取执行时终端界面输出.png "Terminal interface output during video first-frame extraction")

"success" indicates that the extraction is successful. The following shows the comparison of the folder where the video is located before and after. The adapted first frame in mp4 format was successfully extracted and saved as an image in bmp format:

**Figure 4**  Comparison before and after adaptive conversion<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399739789_fig7243352114711"></a>  
![](figures/适配转换前后对比.png "Comparison before and after adaptive conversion")

![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002399621957.png)

### 3D Modeling Tool<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717712"></a>



#### Usage<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412317109"></a>

**Running<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619921_section1992610916599"></a>**

Click Modeling Tools in COMMANDS and select "3D Modeling Tool" at the top.

![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002456945685.png)

**Interface Introduction<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619921_section114256515596"></a>**

-   **Main interface after tool startup**

    After the tool is started, the main interface is shown in the following figure:

    **Figure 1**  Main interface after the 3D modeling tool is started<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619921_fig14212923556"></a>  
    ![](figures/3D建模工具启动主界面.png "Main interface after the 3D modeling tool is started")

    The functions of the buttons are described as follows:

    -   Model type: Select the model to be used (sphere: sphere; mesh: mesh; plane: cylinder). The default parameters are loaded and the model runs automatically.
    -   Texture import: Select the texture material file.
    -   Model import: Select the model file.
    -   Model creation: Open the sub-interface, configure the model parameters, and generate the model.
    -   Model export: Export the model file generated in the corresponding mode of the tool to the local device.
    -   Run: Run the use case in the corresponding mode and display the running effect.
    -   Default parameters: Restore all parameters to their default values with one click.
    -   Parameter export: Adjust the parameters based on the mode configured on the current interface. After obtaining the ideal effect, you can save the group of parameters in this mode to a file.
    -   Show log/Hide log: Turn on/off the log box below.

-   **Model creation interface**

    ① mesh interface: After selecting mesh in the model selection and clicking model creation, the interface shown in the figure is displayed:

    **Figure 2**  mesh interface<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619921_fig179479235556"></a>  
    ![](figures/mesh界面.png "mesh interface")

    ② cylinder interface: After selecting plane in the model selection and clicking model creation, the interface shown in the figure is displayed:

    **Figure 3**  cylinder interface<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619921_fig1460543113556"></a>  
    ![](figures/cylinder界面.png "cylinder interface")

    ③ sphere interface: After selecting sphere in the model selection and clicking model creation, the interface shown in the figure is displayed:

    **Figure 4**  sphere interface<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619921_fig18331542125514"></a>  
    ![](figures/sphere界面.png "sphere interface")

    Click the save button to save the configuration values. After saving, the sub-window closes automatically.

**Operation Steps<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619921_section1371818221405"></a>**

-   **mesh mode:**

    mesh is divided into seven sub-modes. Among them, type0 and type6 are strong interaction modes, in which the model is generated online without offline generation and loading. type1 to type5 are weak interaction modes, in which you need to generate the model offline or load the model before running the use case. You can choose either of the two.

    1.  Click the mesh radio button on the main interface to select the mesh mode.
    2.  Click the model creation button on the main interface to set the model parameters.
    3.  Click the type input box to select the sub-mode and configure the related parameters:

        type0: It is recommended to configure thr as 1.0 and len as 5, configure cx and cy at non-center positions, and keep the other parameters at their default values.

        type1: It is recommended to configure len as 0.0. cx and cy only support configuration at the center point. It is recommended to keep the other parameters at their default values.

        type2: It is recommended to configure len as 4.52. cx and cy only support configuration at the center point. It is recommended to keep the other parameters at their default values.

        type3: It is recommended to configure len as 1. cx and cy only support configuration at the center point. It is recommended to keep the other parameters at their default values.

        type4: It is recommended to configure len as 3. cx and cy only support configuration at the center point. It is recommended to keep the other parameters at their default values.

        type5: It is recommended to configure len as 9.33. cx and cy only support configuration at the center point. It is recommended to keep the other parameters at their default values.

        type6: It is recommended to configure thr as 0.1 and keep the other parameters at their default values.

    4.  Click the save button to save and return to the main interface.
    5.  Click the texture import button on the main interface to select the texture material.
    6.  Click the run button.
    7.  Drag the mesh\_ctrl slider on the right to observe the effect.

-   **cylinder mode:**
    1.  Click the plane radio button on the main interface to select the cylinder mode.
    2.  Click the model creation button on the main interface to set the model parameters. It is recommended to use the default values.
    3.  Click the save button to save and return to the main interface.
    4.  Click the texture import button to select the texture material.
    5.  Click the run button.
    6.  Drag the cylinder\_ctrl slider on the right (mainly slide cylinder\_ctl) to observe the effect.

-   **sphere mode:**
    1.  Click the sphere radio button on the main interface to select the sphere mode.
    2.  Click the model creation button on the main interface to set the model parameters. It is recommended to use the default values.
    3.  Click the save button to save and return to the main interface.
    4.  Click the texture import button to select the texture material.
    5.  Click the run button.
    6.  Drag the three sliders angel\_x, angel\_y, and angel\_z on the right to observe the effect.

**Default Paths of Materials<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002399619921_section88057444012"></a>**

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-notice.gif) **Notice:** 
>The following paths cannot be deleted; otherwise, the program will fail to run.

-   Default texture material path: C:/Users/$\{Username\}/.vscode/extensions/hispark.hisparkstudio-$\{Version\}/dist/source\_file/texture/

-   Default model material path: C:/Users/$\{Username\}/.vscode/extensions/hispark.hisparkstudio-$\{Version\}/dist/source\_file/model/, that is, the default saving path after the model is created.
-   Default mask material path: C:/Users/$\{Username\}/.vscode/extensions/hispark.hisparkstudio-$\{Version\}/dist/source\_file/mask/. This path is used only in the sphere mode. The purpose is to keep a smooth transition at the junction when the texture is superimposed on the model. The width and height of the generated sphere mask must be consistent with dst\_width/dst\_height, and the sphere mask must be ARGB8888.
-   $\{Username\} is the user name of the current Windows login account, and $\{Version\} is the version number of this plug-in.

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-note.gif) **Note:** 
>-   cylinder\_x\_step: logically constrains cylinder\_dst\_width / cylinder\_x\_step
>-   cylinder\_y\_step: logically constrains cylinder\_dst\_height / cylinder\_y\_step
>Note: For the material in cylinder mode, it is recommended that the width be 1/2 of the screen and the height be at least 2048.

### Mask Tool<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412436961"></a>

The mask creation tool supports displaying/generating three types of mask files: circle, Gaussian blur, and HSV.




#### Usage<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717716"></a>

**Running<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366100014_section17318245565"></a>**

Click Modeling Tools in COMMANDS and select "Mask Tool" at the top.

![](figures/zh-cn_image_0000002423227462.png)

**Interface Introduction<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366100014_section1021463835619"></a>**

After the tool is started, the main interface is shown in the following figure:

**Figure 1**  Mask tool startup interface<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366100014_fig39911508561"></a>  
![](figures/蒙版工具启动界面.png "Mask tool startup interface")

The functions of the buttons are described as follows:

-   Model type: Select the model to be used (Gaussian blur mask, circle screenshot mask, HSV mask).
-   Generate mask: Generate the mask image in the left area of the interface.
-   Save mask: Save the mask file.
-   Show log/Hide log: Turn on/off the log box below.

**Operation Steps<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366100014_section17766850105615"></a>**

1.  Select the model type.
2.  Modify the control parameters on the right (optional).
3.  Click "Generate Mask" to view the effect.
4.  Click "Save Mask" to save the mask file.

**Default Path of the Material<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366100014_section1982618594564"></a>**

The generation of the HSV mask depends on the material file. The tool provides a default material file, and you can replace it as needed. The path and file name of the material file are as follows:

C:/Users/$\{Username\}/.vscode/extensions/hispark.hisparkstudio-$\{Version\}/dist/resources/mask/mask\_test/res/input/hsv\_img\_w454\_h454.data

$\{Username\} is the user name of the current Windows login account, and $\{Version\} is the version number of this plug-in.

>![](public_sys-resources/icon-notice.gif) **Notice:** 
>**Note: Do not delete this file or modify its file name; otherwise, the program will fail to run.**

**Method for Generating the Path Parameter of the Gaussian Blur Mask<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002366100014_section710141005719"></a>**

1.  Open the website: [https://www.jyshare.com/more/svgeditor/](https://www.jyshare.com/more/svgeditor/)
2.  Draw the graphics: Currently, only graphics starting with path are supported.
3.  Note: When drawing the graphics, you need to set the resolution on the far right when no graphics are selected.
4.  Click menu bar-\>View-\>Source code.
5.  Copy the string after "d=" in the path to the path.

#### Control Parameters<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412317113"></a>

Path: String (required only in the Gaussian blur mask mode)

Width: width of the material (maximum 640)

Height: height of the material (maximum 600)

Channel: channel of the material (only 3: RGB and 4: ARGB are supported)

#### Precautions<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378877556"></a>

-   Only the Gaussian blur mask requires the path. In the other two modes, the path is empty by default.
-   The circle screenshot mask supports only perfect circles.
-   The HSV mask tool does not support the RGB format currently.

## Common Problems in Using GUI Projects<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412436965"></a>


### Log Record Location of the Started Simulator<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717720"></a>

During the running of the started simulator, the log information is recorded in the "simulator\_build/error.txt" file in the root directory of the currently opened project. For details, see "[Section 11.2.1](Demo运行.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717704)".

# Using Advanced Analysis Functions<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412317117"></a>



## Functions<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378877560"></a>

FBB series projects support the advanced analysis functions, which are as follows:

-   Supports the memory statistics function, which can display the specific memory information of each file in each directory, and supports filtering by memory region and symbol type.
-   Supports saving the current memory statistics as a baseline version and comparing the current memory statistics with the baseline version.
-   Supports exporting the memory statistics as JSON/HTML files. The "export format" and "export path" can be configured in the settings.
-   Supports parsing the CPU trace logs.
-   Supports parsing the CPU trace binary files.
-   Supports parsing the malloc trace logs (applicable to BS20, BS20C, BS21, BS21A, BS21E, BS22, BS25, and BS26 series chips).
-   Supports parsing the IRQ trace logs.

## Function Demonstration<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412436969"></a>






### Memory Statistics Function<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717724"></a>

After the FBB series project is compiled to generate the output directory, click the Analysis button in the lower right corner and select Memory Statistics in the pop-up menu bar (sometimes this window pops up slowly; please wait patiently), as shown in [Memory Statistics](内存统计功能.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717724).

**Figure 1**  Open the memory statistics function<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293383866_fig10508109124015"></a>  
![](figures/打开内存统计功能.png "Open the memory statistics function")

Select the corresponding target to collect memory statistics. The memory statistics interface is shown in [Figure 2](#zh-cn_topic_0000002293383866_fig642651812595).

**Figure 2**  Memory statistics function display<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293383866_fig642651812595"></a>  
![](figures/内存统计功能展示.png "Memory statistics function display")

### Baseline Comparison Function<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412317121"></a>

You can quickly compare the memory usage before and after code changes:

1.  Click the Save as Baseline button to save the current memory as a baseline.
2.  Modify the code or switch the target.
3.  Refresh the statistics.
4.  Click the Compare button, as shown in [Figure 1](#zh-cn_topic_0000002293224194_fig948185817020). An example of the comparison result is shown in [Figure 2](#zh-cn_topic_0000002293224194_fig68321937318).

    **Figure 1**  Buttons for saving the baseline version and comparing with the baseline version<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293224194_fig948185817020"></a>  
    ![](figures/保存基线版本和与基线版本对比按钮.png "Buttons for saving the baseline version and comparing with the baseline version")

    **Figure 2**  Example of the comparison result with the baseline version<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293224194_fig68321937318"></a>  
    ![](figures/与基线版本对比结果示例.png "Example of the comparison result with the baseline version")

### CPU Trace Log Parsing Function<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378877564"></a>

When the chip crashes, the crash log is usually printed through the serial port, which contains the cpu\_trace information (that is, the function call chain for a period of time before the crash).

Following the steps in "[Memory Statistics](内存统计功能.md#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717724)", click the cfbb button in the lower right corner, select Cpu Trace in the pop-up menu bar, select the corresponding target, copy the cpu\_trace log printed during the crash to the text box, and then click the Trace button, as shown in [Figure 1](#zh-cn_topic_0000002327223553_fig2078817371822).

**Figure 1**  Cpu Trace function<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327223553_fig2078817371822"></a>  
![](figures/Cpu-Trace功能.png "Cpu Trace function")

### Heap Memory Usage Statistics (Applicable to BS2X and BS25 Series Chips)<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412436973"></a>

This function can parse the heap memory detection log to quickly analyze the heap memory usage.

1.  Click the Analysis button in the lower right corner.
2.  In the pop-up menu bar, select Malloc Trace, and select the corresponding target. To enable heap memory detection, simply click the button shown in the figure. A rebuild is required. After it is enabled, send the AT command AT+TASKMALLOC= \(the parameter following the command is the task ID\) over the serial port, or call print\_os\_all\_sys\_task\_heap to print the heap memory detection log.

    **Figure 1**  Malloc Trace function<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327183813_fig02211941443"></a>  
    ![](figures/Malloc-Trace功能.png "Malloc Trace function")

3.  Copy the heap memory detection log to the text box.
4.  Click the Trace button to display the statistics chart, as shown in [Figure 2](#zh-cn_topic_0000002327183813_fig1836521310443).

    **Figure 2**  Example of the comparison result with the baseline version<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327183813_fig1836521310443"></a>  
    ![](figures/与基线版本对比结果示例-64.png "Example of the comparison result with the baseline version - 64")

### Detection of Lock Interruption Duration Statistics and Interruption Function Execution Duration Statistics<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717728"></a>

**Enabling Lock Interruption Detection<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293383870_section6996192714500"></a>**

To enable lock interruption detection, add "set\(PUBLIC\_DEFINESOSAL\_IRQ\_RECORD\_INTTER OSAL\_IRQ\_RECORD\_DEBUG\) " at the position shown in the figure in the "kernel/osal\_adapter/CMakeLists.txt" file in the SDK root directory. Note: A rebuild is required.

**Figure 1**  Modification of CMakeLists.txt<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293383870_fig1883953718473"></a>  
![](figures/CMakeLists-txt修改.png "Modification of CMakeLists.txt")

**Using Lock Interruption Detection<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293383870_section470364455017"></a>**

-   Call the "osal\_irq\_record\_flag\_set\(\)" interface to enable or disable lock interruption detection. Its parameter ranges from 0 to 3, and the parameter description is as follows:

    0: Disable interruption detection.

    1: Enable the detection of lock interruption duration statistics.

    2: Enable the detection of interruption function execution duration statistics.

    3: Enable the detection of both lock interruption and interruption function execution duration statistics.

-   Call the "osal\_print\_irq\_record\(\)" interface to print the recorded detection information. Its parameter is empty.

**Parsing the Detection Information<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293383870_section6885916125218"></a>**

1.  Click the cfbb button in the lower right corner.
2.  In the pop-up menu bar, select Irq Trace.
3.  Select the corresponding target.
4.  Copy the detection log to the text box, and click the "Trace" button.

**Figure 2**  Irq Trace function<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293383870_fig15230162419526"></a>  
![](figures/Irq-Trace功能.png "Irq Trace function")

# Common Errors<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412317125"></a>







## The SDK Root Directory Path Is Too Long<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378877568"></a>

The SDK root directory path is too long:

On Windows 10 and Windows 11, the path has a length limit of 260 bytes. An overlong path causes related files to be unavailable during compilation, or the compilation keeps looping certain printed information without executing the actual compilation content. It is recommended to place the SDK code in the root directory of a drive letter, or shorten the path where the SDK is stored.

**Figure 1**  The SDK root directory path is too long<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293383942_fig13895175443715"></a>  
![](figures/SDK根目录路径过长.png "The SDK root directory path is too long")

## Invalid Path<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412436977"></a>

The default paths for debugging, stack analysis, and image analysis become invalid due to problems with the imported project path.

-   Debugging: Modify the default debug\_elf path.

    **Figure 1**  Modify the default debug\_elf path<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293224282_fig1326371603814"></a>  
    ![](figures/修改默认debug_elf路径.png "Modify the default debug_elf path")

## Error Messages About Python or Other Dependencies During Compilation or Execution of System Configuration Functions<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378717732"></a>

Error messages about the Python environment during compilation or execution of system configuration functions:

**Figure 1**  Python environment error dialog box<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327183901_fig155352138378"></a>  
![](figures/python环境出错弹窗.png "Python environment error dialog box")

Or an error message such as "Python is not installed locally" or "The Python installed locally is unavailable" is displayed. The cause is that Python is not installed locally, or the installed Python version is not 3.11.4. To avoid affecting normal compilation or system configuration functions, it is recommended to refer to the Python environment configuration in Chapter 2.4, download the Python 3.11.4 installation package, and manually install it. During the installation, select the "pip" and "tcl/tk options".

Error message about the failure to download a component (XX) during compilation or execution of system configuration functions:

**Figure 2**  Dialog box for the failure to download a pip component<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327183901_fig2582192913375"></a>  
![](figures/pip组件下载失败弹窗.png "Dialog box for the failure to download a pip component")

Alternatively, the download of cmake, pycparser, kconfiglib, or pillow fails. The cause is that the required dependencies fail to be downloaded through the pip component of the installed Python during compilation or execution of system configuration functions. This may be because pip install fails due to the local environment, or the download fails because there is no network connection currently.

Solution: Download the whl file of the corresponding component based on the error message about the failed component. The correspondence between components and download links is as follows:

cmake：[https://files.pythonhosted.org/packages/65/7f/80cf681cd376834b442af8af48e6f17b4197d20b7255aa2f76d8d93a9e44/cmake-3.20.5-py2.py3-none-win\_amd64.whl](https://files.pythonhosted.org/packages/65/7f/80cf681cd376834b442af8af48e6f17b4197d20b7255aa2f76d8d93a9e44/cmake-3.20.5-py2.py3-none-win_amd64.whl)

pycparser：[https://files.pythonhosted.org/packages/62/d5/5f610ebe421e85889f2e55e33b7f9a6795bd982198517d912eb1c76e1a53/pycparser-2.21-py2.py3-none-any.whl](https://files.pythonhosted.org/packages/62/d5/5f610ebe421e85889f2e55e33b7f9a6795bd982198517d912eb1c76e1a53/pycparser-2.21-py2.py3-none-any.whl)

kconfiglib：[https://files.pythonhosted.org/packages/8a/f1/d98a89231e779b079b977590efcc31249d959c8f1d4b5858cad69695ff9c/kconfiglib-14.1.0-py2.py3-none-any.whl](https://files.pythonhosted.org/packages/8a/f1/d98a89231e779b079b977590efcc31249d959c8f1d4b5858cad69695ff9c/kconfiglib-14.1.0-py2.py3-none-any.whl)

windows\_curses：[https://files.pythonhosted.org/packages/18/1b/e06eb41dad1c74f0d3124218084f258f73a5e76c67112da0ba174162670f/windows\_curses-2.3.3-cp311-cp311-win\_amd64.whl](https://files.pythonhosted.org/packages/18/1b/e06eb41dad1c74f0d3124218084f258f73a5e76c67112da0ba174162670f/windows_curses-2.3.3-cp311-cp311-win_amd64.whl)

pillow：

[https://files.pythonhosted.org/packages/c1/d0/5866318eec2b801cdb8c82abf190c8343d8a1cd8bf5a0c17444a6f268291/pillow-10.4.0-cp311-cp311-win\_amd64.whl](https://files.pythonhosted.org/packages/c1/d0/5866318eec2b801cdb8c82abf190c8343d8a1cd8bf5a0c17444a6f268291/pillow-10.4.0-cp311-cp311-win_amd64.whl)

After the download is complete, open the command prompt (cmd) in the directory where the file is located (it is recommended to store it in the installation directory of Python 3.11.4), and execute the command "pip install  the corresponding whl file". Take cmake and windows\_curses as an example:

**Figure 3**  Example of downloading whl files<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327183901_fig8902128123817"></a>  
![](figures/下载whl文件举例.png "Example of downloading whl files")

Commands to execute:

**Figure 4**  Executing the pip install command<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327183901_fig1058514345386"></a>  
![](figures/执行pip-install命令.png "Executing the pip install command")

To check whether the execution is successful, for cmake, open the command prompt and run "cmake --version". If the corresponding version number is displayed, cmake is downloaded successfully. For other components, run "pip show component name". If information about the corresponding component is displayed, the dependencies are downloaded successfully:

**Figure 5**  Checking that the dependencies are downloaded successfully<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327183901_fig15897171463910"></a>  
![](figures/依赖下载成功检测.png "Checking that the dependencies are downloaded successfully")

If all environments are configured successfully but the compilation still fails with the error "is the command line too long", the cmake version does not match the required version. You need to download cmake-3.20.5-py2.py3-none-win\_amd64.whl in the installation directory of Python 3.11.4 and run "pip install  the downloaded cmake whl file" in this directory.

Download link:

[https://files.pythonhosted.org/packages/65/7f/80cf681cd376834b442af8af48e6f17b4197d20b7255aa2f76d8d93a9e44/cmake-3.20.5-py2.py3-none-win\_amd64.whl](https://files.pythonhosted.org/packages/65/7f/80cf681cd376834b442af8af48e6f17b4197d20b7255aa2f76d8d93a9e44/cmake-3.20.5-py2.py3-none-win_amd64.whl)

**Figure 6**  Error message: is the command line too long<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327183901_fig57321230113917"></a>  
![](figures/提示is-the-command-line-too-long.png "Error message: is the command line too long")

If the Python environment is normal but the pip install component fails:

**Figure 7**  Failure to execute pip install<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327183901_fig10490436153913"></a>  
![](figures/执行pip-install失败.png "Failure to execute pip install")

The cause is that the pip version is inconsistent with the Python version. That is, the environment variable of Python 3.11.4 or another compliant Python version is added to the environment variables, but the environment variable of Python311\\Scripts in the corresponding Python installation directory is not added. You can manually add the environment variable of Python\\Scripts to the head of the environment variables. After the pip dependencies are downloaded successfully, close all VS Code windows and open them again.

**Figure 8**  Adding the environment variable of Python311<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002327183901_fig227546143910"></a>  
![](figures/添加python311的环境变量.png "Adding the environment variable of Python311")

## Error "NameError: name 're' is not defined" Reported for Searching in the Kconfig Jump to Dialog Box<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412317129"></a>

1.  After opening Kconfig, click the "Jump to..." button and search for the related content in the dialog box.

    **Figure 1**  Opening the search box from Jump to<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293383962_fig252132311619"></a>  
    ![](figures/从Jump-to打开搜索框.png "Opening the search box from Jump to")

2.  If the following abnormal print appears:

    **Figure 2**  Abnormal print of Kconfig<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293383962_fig17981081134"></a>  
    ![](figures/Kconfig异常打印.png "Abnormal print of Kconfig")

    You need to modify the "guiconfig.py" file: before calling the re module, add import re, as shown in [Figure 3](#zh-cn_topic_0000002293383962_fig23065516141).

    **Figure 3**  Adding import re<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293383962_fig23065516141"></a>  
    ![](figures/添加import-re.png "Adding import re")

3.  After the code is added, the search can be performed normally, as shown in [Figure 4](#zh-cn_topic_0000002293383962_fig1265846121315).

    **Figure 4**  Schematic diagram of normal search<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002293383962_fig1265846121315"></a>  
    ![](figures/正常搜索示意图.png "Schematic diagram of normal search")

## Compilation Error "Invalid argument"<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002378877572"></a>

If the error "Invalid argument" is reported during compilation.

**Figure 1**  Compilation error "Invalid argument" of ws63<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002304197682_fig1489361241813"></a>  
![](figures/ws63编译报错-Invalid-argument.png "Compilation error: Invalid argument of ws63")

Cause: The elf file cannot be parsed because of the lack of administrator permissions. Solution: Open VS Code with administrator permissions and compile again.

## Error Reported by the Python Environment Check<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002412436981"></a>

**Figure 1**  Error reported by the Python environment check<a name="zh-cn_topic_0000002339901405_fig1449715297549"></a>  
![](figures/Python环境检查报错.jpg "Error reported by the Python environment check")

If an error is reported during Python installation, as shown in the preceding figure, you can run "Python --version" in cmd and check the environment variables to verify whether Python is installed correctly. If the problem is still not resolved, check whether other plug-ins installed in VS Code conflict with it, and you can disable the other plug-ins for troubleshooting.
